### [1] [Chern Numbers from Symmetry-Enforced Zeros and Dispersion Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25028)
*Yoonseok Hwang,Henry Davenport,Frank Schindler*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用陈能带与参考态之间的波函数重叠，证明晶体对称性可强制重叠零点并固定其涡度（模转动阶数），且弱局域微扰产生的色散移动正比于重叠的模方，从而无需测量波函数相位即可从色散读出零点结构、识别陈拓扑，并给出零点处量子度规的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Berry曲率是刻画陈拓扑的常用手段，但需要测量波函数相位。本文寻求一种互补的、无需相位信息的表征方式：利用波函数零点及其局域涡度来刻画陈拓扑。

Method: 构造陈能带与任意自由选取参考态之间的波函数重叠；结合晶体对称性分析重叠零点的存在性与涡度（由能带与参考态的角动量决定，模转动阶数）；分析弱局域微扰引起的能带色散移动，证明其正比于重叠模方；推广至多分量、多能带波函数及动量表象在部分动量处为零的参考态（如紧致局域态）。

Result: 晶体对称性可强制重叠零点并固定其涡度模转动阶数；弱局域微扰引起的色散移动∝|重叠|²，使零点与局域结构可直接从色散读出；结合对称性可在不测相位的情况下识别陈拓扑；零点附近的色散移动给出该点量子度规的下界。

Conclusion: 波函数重叠零点及其局域涡度构成陈拓扑的互补表征，可在不测量波函数相位的条件下借助晶体对称性与色散测量识别陈数，并适用于多分量/多能带体系及紧致局域态等参考态，同时提供量子度规的下界估计。

Abstract: Wave-function zeros and their local vorticities provide a complementary characterization of Chern topology to more commonly used Berry-curvature formulations. Using wave-function overlaps between a Chern band and freely chosen reference states, we show that crystalline symmetry can enforce their zeros and fix their vorticities modulo the rotation order from the angular momenta of the band and reference state. We further show that weak local perturbations produce band-dispersion shifts proportional to the absolute square of the overlap, allowing its zeros and local structure to be read off directly from the dispersion. Combined with crystalline symmetry, this can identify Chern topology without measuring wave-function phases. Our construction applies to multicomponent and multiband wave functions and allows reference states whose momentum-space representation may vanish at some momenta. This permits compact localized states to be used directly as reference states and makes use of their known real- and momentum-space properties in flat-band systems. The dispersion shift near an overlap zero further provides a lower bound on the quantum metric at the zero.

</details>


### [2] [Topological (in)equivalence encoded in zeros of wavefunction overlaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25037)
*Carolina Paiva,Bruno Mera,Roni Ilan,Raquel Queiroz*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出用两个占据子空间之间重叠矩阵的行列式来统一刻画Chern数与Z₂拓扑的"相对拓扑"，其零点携带相位缠绕定义的整数荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两条能带结构的拓扑等价性取决于占据态能否在不开能隙、不破坏保护对称性的条件下连续形变。现有Chern数与Z₂不变量分属不同框架，缺乏统一的、可直接从两系统比较中读出相对拓扑的表述。

Method: 构造两个占据子空间之间的重叠矩阵，以其行列式定义布里渊区上线丛的一个全局截面；在映射不可逆处该截面为零，并引入曲率集中于零点的奇异联络，用零点的相位缠绕赋予整数荷。分别在2D class A与class AII中分析总荷与相对不变量，并结合晶格对称性约束零点位置与荷，最后用Haldane、Kane-Mele、Qi-Wu-Zhang模型演示。

Result: 2D class A中总荷等于两体系Chern数之差；class AII中时间反演将等量异号荷配对，相对Z₂不变量为半个布里渊区内总荷的奇偶性，Kane-Mele Pfaffian作为特例被复原；零点与两体系插值过程的开能隙点重合，旋转对称性可单独强制零点存在。

Conclusion: 重叠矩阵行列式的零点图像为Chern与Z₂不变量提供了单一几何框架，将相对拓扑、能隙闭合位置与晶体对称性约束统一起来。

Abstract: Topological equivalence between two gapped band structures is determined by whether their occupied states can be continuously deformed into one another without closing the energy gap or breaking the protecting symmetries. We show that this relative topology can be read directly from the overlap matrix between the two occupied subspaces, in a way that treats Chern and $\mathbb{Z}_2$ invariants within a single framework. The determinant of the overlap matrix defines a global section of a line bundle over the Brillouin zone, which vanishes at the momenta where the map between the subspaces fails to be invertible. A singular connection, whose curvature is concentrated at these zeros, assigns each zero an integer charge given by its phase winding. In two-dimensional class A, the total charge equals the difference between the Chern numbers of the two systems. In class AII, time reversal pairs zeros of opposite charge, and the relative $\mathbb{Z}_2$ invariant is the parity of the total charge in half the Brillouin zone; the Kane-Mele Pfaffian is recovered as a special case. The zeros coincide with the gap closings of an interpolation between the two systems, and crystalline symmetries constrain their positions and charges. We illustrate the construction in the Haldane, Kane-Mele, and Qi-Wu-Zhang models, including cases where rotation symmetry alone enforces the zeros.

</details>


### [3] [Circuit model for gate-induced excitation of quantum Hall edges tuned by DC gate bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25332)
*Kodai Sugizaki,Takaaki Mano,Go Yusa*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究栅极诱导边缘激发的输入电压—激发波形关系，发现随栅偏置电压跨越夹断电压，激发在时域由类一阶导数响应变为正比于输入波形，并可用单一等效电路模型（两侧不同有效栅—2DEG电容）统一描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子霍尔体系边缘激发在低维电子系统与介观输运研究中备受关注，但栅极诱导激发的输入电压与激发波形之间的定量关系尚不清楚，尤其缺乏对栅偏置依赖机制的统一描述。

Method: 实验测量栅极诱导边缘激发的时域与频域响应，通过改变栅极偏置电压（offset voltage）系统扫描激发波形随偏置的演化；建立单一等效电路模型，在夹断电压两侧采用不同的有效栅—2DEG电容进行拟合，并与器件结构计算电容对照。

Result: 时域上，激发波形随栅偏置从类一阶导数响应过渡为与输入波形成正比的响应；频域上，栅激发的频率依赖随偏置跨越夹断电压发生显著变化；单一等效电路模型配以夹断电压两侧不同的有效电容可描述上述全部变化，且拟合所得有效电容与器件结构计算值一致。

Conclusion: 栅极诱导边缘激发的波形变换由有效栅—2DEG电容随夹断状态的改变所主导，可用统一等效电路模型定量刻画，为量子霍尔边缘激发的波形调控与介观输运实验设计提供依据。

Abstract: Edge excitations, which propagate along the edge of a quantum Hall system, are of great interest in the study of low-dimensional electron systems and mesoscopic transport phenomena. In this study, we investigate the relationship between the input voltage and the excitation waveform for gate-induced excitations. By varying the offset voltage applied to the gate, we observe that the excitation waveform changes from a first-derivative-like response to a response proportional to the input waveform in the time domain. In the frequency domain, we observe that the frequency dependence of the gate excitation changes significantly as the offset voltage crosses the pinch-off voltage. We show that these changes can be described by a single equivalent circuit model with different effective gate-2DEG capacitances on either side of the pinch-off voltage. In addition, we confirm that the effective capacitance obtained from the fitting of the model with the experimental data is consistent with the capacitance calculated from the device structure.

</details>


### [4] [Topological Bound States in the Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25493)
*Dan-Na Liu,J. Villavicencio,Pierre A. Pantaleon*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在AB堆叠双层石墨烯的质量壁结中发现了拓扑束缚态于连续谱（BIC），其沿壁传播由谷拓扑支撑，而束缚性质由限制条件的绕数决定，可在不改变谷拓扑的情况下通过调节结长或层间偏压而解除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 界面态传播与束缚态形成通常被分别归因于拓扑，但二者的互补关系及束缚态如何在导电连续谱中完全与引线解耦尚缺乏统一图像。

Method: 研究AB堆叠双层石墨烯中的质量壁结（mass-wall junction），结合谷拓扑分析与一维限制条件的绕数（winding）判定，并考察结长度与层间偏压对束缚态能级与共振宽度的影响。

Result: 发现沿壁传播的界面态由谷拓扑保护；限制条件的非平庸绕数导致与导电引线的精确解耦（真正的BIC）。调节结长或层间偏压可使带相反电荷的束缚态合并、解除限制而不改变谷拓扑；在一般临界点处态仍保持束缚，邻近共振宽度随动量以四次方（quartic）开启。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了拓扑在“界面传播”与“连续谱中束缚”两方面的互补作用，为可调控的拓扑束缚态与共振提供了平台。

Abstract: We uncover topological bound states in the continuum in AB-stacked bilayer graphene mass-wall junctions. Valley topology supports interface states propagating along the wall, while a distinct winding of the confinement conditions governs exact decoupling from conducting leads. Tuning the junction length or interlayer bias can merge oppositely charged bound states and remove confinement without changing the valley topology. At generic critical points, the state remains bound and nearby resonance widths open quartically with momentum. These results connect complementary roles of topology in interface propagation and confinement within a conducting continuum.

</details>


### [5] [Field-free deterministic spin-orbit torque magnetization switching using monolayer WTe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25755)
*Ganesh Hanchanahal,Arnav Jain,Shrushti Tapar,Saroj P Dash,Bhaskaran Muralidharan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文基于非平衡格林函数框架建模交换耦合宏自旋，研究单层WTe₂（1T_d与1T'相）作为自旋源驱动的无场确定型磁化翻转，比较两相及条带取向、磁层宽度、偏压对翻转动力学的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋轨道矩磁存储（SOT-MRAM）走向实用化的关键障碍是磁化矢量的无场翻转，需要具备必要对称性的自旋源材料来实现无场确定型翻转。

Method: 对单层WTe₂的1T_d与1T'两相进行对比分析；将铁磁层建模为交换耦合宏自旋，置于非平衡格林函数（NEGF）框架中，计算电流诱导自旋极化及相应的类阻尼矩，并研究偏压、条带取向与磁层宽度对翻转动力学的影响。

Result: 1T_d与1T'两相均可实现翻转；1T_d相的x-ribbon几何产生的电流诱导自旋极化显著大于1T'相，从而对相邻磁层施加更大的类阻尼矩并实现更快翻转。1T'器件的翻转时间随磁层宽度强烈且非单调变化，而1T_d器件在较宽范围内近乎恒定，符合边缘主导力矩的特征。

Conclusion: x-ribbon 1T_d-WTe₂是有望用于无场SOT-MRAM的自旋源材料。

Abstract: An important step towards the practical implementation of spin-orbit torque magnetic memory is the field-free switching of the magnetization vector. In this work, we investigate spin-orbit torque-driven magnetization dynamics and switching devices comprising monolayer $\mathrm{WTe}_2$ as the spin-source material. We perform a comparative analysis of the two phases of $\mathrm{WTe}_2$, namely the $1\mathrm{T}_d$ and $1\mathrm{T}'$ with the necessary symmetries for field-free deterministic magnetization switching. We demonstrate switching in both systems and analyse the dependence of the switching dynamics on the applied bias voltage, ribbon orientation and width of the magnetic layer. Modelling the ferromagnet as an exchange-coupled macrospin within a non-equilibrium Green's function framework, we find that the $x$-ribbon geometry of the $1\mathrm{T}_d$ $\mathrm{WTe}_2$ phase generates a significantly larger current-induced spin polarization than the $1\mathrm{T}'$ phase, resulting in a larger damping-like torque on the adjacent magnetic layer and faster switching. The switching time of $1\mathrm{T}'$ devices varies strongly and non-monotonically with the width of the magnetic layer. In $1\mathrm{T}_d$ devices, it remains nearly constant over a broad range of widths, consistent with an edge-dominated torque. These results identify $x$-ribbon $1\mathrm{T}_d$-$\mathrm{WTe}_2$ as a promising spin source for field-free SOT-MRAM.

</details>


### [6] [Helices at Twisted Ferroelectric Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25828)
*Kristina Holsgrove,Ronan Lynch,Conor McCluskey,Eoin Moynihan,Andrew Rogers,Xinxin Hu,Boris Croes,Loan Hajdari-Manigault,Maria Lazarut,Eadaoin Anderson,Sam Seddon,Anna Razumnaya,Yurii Tikhonov,Ana M. Sanchez,Marios Hadjimichael,Amit Kumar,Raymond McQuaid,Jordi Arbiol,Gustau Catalan,Richard Beanland,Igor Lukyanchuk,Lukas M. Eng,Salia Cherifi-Hertel,Tchavdar Todorov,J. Marty Gregg*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 孪晶界面处双铁电铌酸锂单晶形成由螺型位错分隔的相干键合纳米结构螺旋阵列，螺旋引发扭转应变进而产生偶极螺旋，表现为手性并用DPC翻转不变性与SHG圆二色性证实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究扭转铁电氧化物界面（区别于二维范德华扭转堆垛）的原子结构本质，澄清其是否为莫尔条纹式晶格叠加，并考察扭转应变与铁电极化的耦合。

Method: 4D STEM与差分相位对比成像、序列扫描透射电子显微学电子通道轨迹建模、电子叠层成像（ptychography）直接成像、结合压电张量与挠曲电系数推断偶极螺旋，以及二次谐波圆偏振二色性测量。

Result: 界面含相干键合、弹性变形的纳米结构螺旋，被螺型位错分隔；4D STEM/DPC像中呈现显著衬度并经模型解释；叠层成像直接确认螺旋结构；扭转应变导致偶极螺旋形成；DPC像在样品上下翻转前后一致且SHG圆二色性信号异常大，共同证实手性。

Conclusion: 结构螺旋具有拓扑上与涡旋不同的手性特征，并非莫尔条纹，而是伴随塑性与弹性变形的晶格旋转；紧密键合的扭转氧化物界面特性迥异于二维范德华扭转堆垛，开辟了新的"twistronics"方向。

Abstract: We report that twisted interfaces, formed between two ferroelectric lithium niobate single crystals, contain arrays of coherently bonded and elastically deformed nanoscale structural helices, separated from one another by screw dislocations. The helices generate remarkable contrast in four dimensional scanning transmission electron microscopy (4D STEM) and through related differential phase contrast (DPC). Such images have been successfully rationalised by modelling the trajectory of electrons that channel along helical pathways as they traverse the interface, but the structural helices implied have also been directly imaged using electron ptychography. Importantly, as lithium niobate is a uniaxial ferroelectric, the torsional strain in these structural helices necessarily also causes dipolar helices to form, inferred through consideration of pertinent piezoelectric tensors and flexoelectric coefficients. Helices are topologically distinct from the more commonly reported vortices and, importantly, they are chiral. Helical (and hence chiral) character was confirmed by observing that DPC images were identical before and after the sample was inverted. Chirality was also established through second harmonic optical circularly-polarised dichroism measurements, in which unusually large interfacial signals were seen. It must be stressed that the arrays of helices observed are distinct from moiré patterns; they facilitate lattice rotation combining plastic and elastic deformation and are not simply a superposition of lattices, twisted with respect to one another. Thus, intimately bonded twisted oxide interfaces will have distinctly different characteristics from those of twisted stacks of two-dimensional van der Waals materials, opening up a new and distinct kind of twistronics.

</details>


### [7] [Higher-order magnetic hopfions in uniaxial chiral magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25883)
*Vladyslav M. Kuchkin,Nikolai S. Kiselev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文证明单轴各向异性可稳定一族具有零或任意正Hopf指数的打结与链环hopfion，并给出基于skyrmion编织映射的系统构造方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性磁体中虽理论上存在hopfion，但实验观测到的构型基本局限于hopfion环、heliknoton及其团簇，缺乏对更一般拓扑结构稳定存在的机制与构造手段。

Method: 在单轴各向异性—外场参数空间中确定各类打结/链环hopfion的稳定区域；建立将skyrmion编织（braid）映射到三维磁结构的系统构造框架。

Result: 获得零及任意正Hopf指数的打结与链环hopfion稳定区域，并发现稳定hopfion的最小能量随Hopf指数近似线性增长。

Conclusion: 单轴各向异性是稳定多样打结hopfion的有效调控手段，skyrmion编织映射为设计高Hopf指数拓扑磁结构提供通用途径。

Abstract: Chiral magnets host hopfions, but their experimentally observed configurations are largely limited to hopfion rings, heliknotons, and their clusters. Here, we show that uniaxial anisotropy stabilizes a diverse family of knotted and linked hopfions with zero and arbitrary positive Hopf indices. We identify their stability regions in the anisotropy -- field parameter space and introduce a systematic construction based on mapping skyrmion braids into three-dimensional magnetic textures. We find that the minimum energy of stable hopfions increases approximately linearly with the Hopf index.

</details>


### [8] [Topology-Enabled Self-Verification of Quantum Hall Resistance Standards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26016)
*DaVonne Henry,Dominick S. Scaletta,Tamar M. Padwa,Heather M. Hill,Gretchen B. Ferguson,Evelyn E. Hopkins,Mattias Kruskopf,Dean G. Jarrett,David B. Newell,Albert F. Rigosi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出"对称精密量子化可靠性"（SPQR）判据，仅通过Cn对称器件的外端测量即可统计性地确保多元素量子化霍尔阵列电阻标准内部量子化未失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元素量子化霍尔阵列电阻标准的规模化，需要严格验证内部量子化未发生击穿，但又不希望依赖耗散边缘态输运的验证。

Method: 构建数学框架：基于Cn对称设计的仅外端测量，用统计方法界定隐藏局域缺陷（源于量子霍尔击穿）出现的概率上界。

Result: 该判据可给出隐藏局域缺陷似然性的界，从而在不测量边缘态输运的前提下验证内部量子化可靠性；潜在可用于量子计算与半导体测试。

Conclusion: SPQR判据为多元素量子化霍尔阵列标准提供了一种统计可靠的验证途径，文中以其在量子电学标准中的应用为主要背景进行阐述。

Abstract: The scaling of multi-element quantized Hall array resistance standards necessitates rigorous validation that internal quantization has not broken down without relying on the verification of dissipationless edge state transport. One proposed solution is to use a Symmetric Precision Quantization Reliability criterion, a mathematical framework that statistically assures such validation strictly through outer terminal measurements of Cn-symmetric designs. This criterion essentially bounds the likelihood of hidden localized defects arising from quantum Hall breakdown and has potential uses in quantum computing and semiconductor testing but is presented in the context of its application toward quantum electrical standards.

</details>


### [9] [Investigating Thermal Boundary Conductance in Monolayer Transition Metal Dichalcogenides: A Systematic Comparison of Advanced Nanoscale Thermometry Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26129)
*Timm Swoboda,Jiaqi Yang,Arkadiusz P. Gertych,Carlos Acosta,Onurcan Kaya,Daniel Capolat Palomar,Severine Gomès,Aitor Lopeandia,Stephan Roche,Javier Rodriguez-Viejo,Marianna Sledzinska*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文对四种蓝宝石衬底上的单层过渡金属硫族化物（TMD）开展拉曼热测量、扫描热显微镜（SThM）与频域热反射（FDTR）三种技术的交叉对比研究，基于同一批样品排除样品间差异，揭示了TBC测量值的材料依赖性与方法依赖性，并厘清各表征方法的不确定度来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维器件散热依赖热边界导（TBC），但文献中单层TMD等二维材料的TBC报道值相差一个数量级以上，难以判断差异源于材料本征性质还是测量方法。缺乏在相同样品上进行的横向对比基准。

Abstract: Thermal boundary conductance (TBC) plays a crucial role in heat dissipation in two-dimensional (2D) devices. However, reported values for 2D material classes such as monolayer transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDs) can vary by more than an order of magnitude across the literature, making it difficult to determine whether these differences come from the materials themselves or from the measurement methods used. To address this issue, this article presents a systematic, cross-technique study of the interfacial heat transport in four TMD monolayers supported on a sapphire substrate, using three of the most representative methods: Raman thermometry, scanning thermal microscopy (SThM) and frequency domain thermoreflectance (FDTR). By conducting all measurements on the same sets of samples, the influence of sample-to-sample variation is minimized allowing for a direct comparison of the three approaches. The results reveal a variation in the measured values depending on both the material and the measurement technique. By directly comparing these results we can elucidate on the origin of the differences and underpin sources of uncertainty in each of the characterisation methods. Taken together, these results provide a direct experimental benchmark for studies of 2D material interfaces and offer practical guidance for the selection and interpretation of thermal metrology.

Abstract: Local currents carry the orbital magnetization of a macroscopic sample and are key for modeling inhomogeneous magnetic materials and for understanding magnetoelectric and topological responses. This eliminates the need for independent simulations previously required for off-diagonal elements, which significantly speeds up the calculation of spatial maps of local currents. Further, we express bond currents in terms of loop currents that obey a discrete Poisson equation, with the current vorticity acting as the source term. Its solution yields a local orbital magnetization. In analogy with the global Středa relation, the derivative of this magnetization with respect to the chemical potential defines a new local Chern marker. Built entirely from bond currents, it requires no position operator and is not bound by the zero-sum rule of projector markers in open samples. Abstract: Entanglement entropies of pure multipartite states can be constrained by organizing subsystem cuts as edges (hyperedges) of graphs (hypergraphs). Starting from the one-party entanglement polygon inequality, we derive these constraints from strong subadditivity and pure-state complementarity, with optimal coefficients and explicit equality conditions. A refinement involving three mutual information terms gives the exact minimum of the weighted sum at fixed entropies of the two parties and of their union, attained by at most three independent bipartite pure states. This criterion recovers the cycle and complete-graph inequalities and shows that uniform incidence parity, which we prove sufficient for polygon inequalities on hypergraphs, is not necessary. We characterize pure chain equality states, bound the trace distance to cycle equality states independently of the number of parties and local dimensions, and obtain bond-dimension bounds for matrix product states and sequential-recovery error estimates.

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing needs logical states prepared such that single faults cannot spread into uncorrectable errors. Existing automated methods, however, each use one check construction and yield fixed trade-offs.

Abstract: Quantum simulators have become a reality in recent years, with several platforms now remotely accessible through the cloud. Abstract: Diffusion maps generalize principal component analysis (PCA) to nonlinear manifolds, enabling spectral dimensionality reduction, geometric data analysis, and elliptic PDE approximation on point clouds. Abstract: In this paper, additional properties of a previously introduced non-linear extension of quantum mechanics are investigated. Abstract: We propose a route to prepare entangled states of microwaves propagating in spatially separated transmission lines using the intrinsic nonlinear dynamics of ferromagnetic spin waves (magnons). Our work establishes that quantum states of propagating magnons can be created by their nonlinear dynamics alone, and opens a pathway toward integrating magnons in hybrid quantum platforms.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作假设可用量子计算机端到端求解 PDE 约束设计问题：目标被块编码而非测量，由 QHD 直接消耗。但该管线依赖两个未经验证的假设——oracle 构造要求仿射生成元具正半定厄米部分，以及摩擦启发式对目标常数平移不变——二者在协方差型目标上均不成立，需识别并修复。

Abstract: Networked quantum technologies allow users to operate quantum machines hosted by remote servers, but classical commands alone cannot ensure that only the intended user can prepare the useful inputs that activate them. We formulate the problem of adversarial remote use of quantum machines in the framework of quantum resource theories. A device controlled by Bob is correlated to a remote user, Alice, through a bipartite state, and also to an adversary Eve holding its purification. Alice and Eve compete to programme Bob's machine by remotely preparing its inputs: the conditional state carries the quantum resource, while the measurement outcome selects the machine operation. Remote use is truly advantageous when Alice is guaranteed to unlock a larger average task value than the adversary Eve, given the same marginal state of Bob. We show that for tasks with a convex figure of merit, the competition is governed by complementary upper roofs and, for spectral tasks, where the figure of merit is invariant under unitary operations, by a corresponding entanglement imbalance. Purification, work extraction, quantum batteries, and coherence distillation show that the same noisy state may secure exclusivity one machine but not another. Finally, we order Alice's and Eve's complete programming powers through a task-independent simulation preorder, based on the ensembles each party can prepare on Bob's side. Shared quantum correlations thereby become a key resource for controlling access to remote quantum machines.

Abstract: We study the computational complexity of estimating observables and sampling from stationary states, or fixed points, of bosonic Lindbladian dynamics with bounded-degree polynomial interactions. While the complexity of transient bosonic dynamics has been investigated, understanding the computational power of bosonic fixed points requires addressing subtleties of infinite-dimensional systems, including the existence, uniqueness, and convergence of stationary states.
  We establish a broad complexity landscape spanning universal quantum computation, efficient classical simulation, sampling hardness, and undecidability. First, we show that universal quantum computation can be embedded into observable estimation at the unique fixed point of a bosonic Lindbladian with a Hamiltonian of degree at most twelve and only linear photon loss. Moreover, this fixed point can be prepared from vacuum in polynomial time. On the classical side, we identify Gaussian regimes with incoherent pumping that admit efficient photon-number sampling and establish stability of classical approximability under sufficiently weak non-Gaussian Kerr perturbations. Conversely, for Gaussian fixed points without incoherent pumping, we establish hardness of exact sampling under constant-rate linear loss and hardness of approximate sampling at inverse-polynomial loss rates under standard boson-sampling conjectures. We also relate sampling from non-Gaussian fixed points to variants of boson sampling. Finally, we prove that deciding the existence of a stationary density operator is $\mathsf{RE}$-hard, even for purely dissipative Lindbladians with degree-five jump operators acting on at most four modes.

Abstract: We introduce a unified framework for sensing time-dependent fields through the lens of classical and quantum algorithms. First, we develop three pulse sequence protocols, namely randomized, matched, and pooled spectroscopy, which outperform established approaches such as dynamical decoupling across a wide range of tasks. These protocols attain the optimal sensitivity for linear and nonlinear AC sensing on conventional spin or qubit platforms, improving existing devices by changing only their pulse sequences. We then introduce quantum computational spectroscopy (QCS), which couples quantum sensors to a quantum processor. QCS combines two new primitives: a quantum computational frequency comb that coherently converts any classical signal into a quantum oracle, and a family of quantum algorithms that use the oracle to extract signal information in superposition. Variations can be implemented on current platforms with only one memory ancilla. We show that the ultimate limit imposed by quantum mechanics lies polynomially beyond the Heisenberg limit, and that QCS achieves it. Attaining this new limit requires quantum computation, establishing a fundamental separation between conventional and quantum computational sensing. Moreover, QCS retains beyond-Heisenberg scaling for broadband signals under constant-strength sensor depolarization, while we prove that the same noise limits any protocol lacking quantum computation to standard-quantum-limit scaling. Our result provides the first asymptotic, beyond-quadratic quantum advantage in AC metrology when noise is aligned with the signal, proving that Lindblad span conditions do not apply to broadband quantum sensing. Our work builds an algorithmic theory for designing sensing protocols and establishes quantum sensing as a new frontier for quantum algorithms, with quantum advantages that surpass the Heisenberg limit and that persist under noise.

</details>

However, problem-specific information can reduce the number of samples needed to obtain the required answer. We investigate this possibility in supervised quantum machine learning, where labeled examples provide information about the function to be learned. Binary classification provides such a setting, since correct prediction depends on the sign and margin of the classifier output rather than on accurate reconstruction of every noiseless expectation value. We develop a theoretical and empirical framework for comparing boosting of noisy parameterized quantum circuits with probabilistic error cancellation (PEC), requiring both methods to reach the same learning target. For the specified circuit architectures under Pauli noise, we establish a common hypothesis-class complexity bound: matching empirical margin loss gives the same upper bound on expected classification risk. We derive resource criteria and evaluate AdaBoostR and Real AdaBoost in finite-shot teacher-student simulations across circuit depth and physical noise strength. Under the same classification-accuracy target, boosting requires fewer circuit executions as depth and noise increase. Boosting also achieves higher test accuracy than the corresponding reference. For the supervised-learning problems studied, these results demonstrate a task-dependent resource advantage: exploiting information retained in noisy outputs can avoid the cost of ideal-output reconstruction without sacrificing observed classification performance.

Abstract: Differential privacy has been successfully extended to quantum computing and information processing, where sensitive data are encoded in quantum states and protected by quantum noise mechanisms that keep the outcomes of any measurement on neighboring states nearly indistinguishable. In the quantum setting, however, the state submitted to a quantum computer may be entangled with a hidden reference system secretly held by an adversary. Such entanglement cannot be detected from the submitted state alone, and it poses a challenge with no counterpart in the classical setting. Existing frameworks of quantum differential privacy consider only measurements on the released state, leaving this scenario largely unexplored.

We show that this attack may completely break a mechanism that satisfies quantum differential privacy with a finite parameter, driving its privacy parameter to infinity. To counter it, we propose entanglement-assisted quantum differential privacy (EA-QDP), a privacy framework that is native to quantum entanglement and remains valid against such attacks. We characterize exactly when a mechanism has a finite EA-QDP parameter, and show that the optimal parameter $\varepsilon^*_{\mathrm{EA}}$ of any quantum mechanism can be computed by a semidefinite programming problem (SDP). Building on this, we quantify the extra privacy leakage caused by entanglement and show that it can be bounded by choosing appropriate mechanisms, with the depolarizing channel being our recommended choice. Finally, we demonstrate both the attack and its countermeasure on simulated and physical quantum hardware, and the experimental results agree with our theoretical predictions.

One makes 23% fewer logical errors than BP+OSD-0 with damping at 0.64$\times$ its time, and decoders evolved from random code are more accurate than ldpc's tuned BP+OSD-0 at 1/31 of its time or less. Frozen at one operating point, the two fastest evolved decoders generalize well across noise levels, code sizes and round counts, staying at the time-error front. Ultimately, we demonstrate that capturing expert knowledge within a DSL creates a powerful intermediate layer---one that effectively bridges human intuition and machine-driven optimization to unlock highly efficient quantum error correction.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在纯化量子查询访问下，以 Õ(n^{u_k}/ε^{1-u_k} + n^{(k-1)/(2k)}ε) 次查询实现k阶Rényi熵的加性ε估计，其中 u_k = max_{2≤j≤k} [(3k-j)2^{j-2}-k]/[k(2^j-1)]。

Conclusion: 递归碰撞态复用与阈值式幂和分解相结合，可有效降低整数阶Rényi熵估计的量子查询复杂度，且无需幂和的先验知识。

Abstract: Entropy estimation for unknown distributions is a basic problem in statistical property estimation. Given purified quantum query-access to a distribution on $n$ symbols, our algorithm estimates its order-$k$ Rényi entropy to within additive error $ε$ using $$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{n^{u_k}}{ε^{1-u_k}} + \frac{n^{(k-1)/(2k)}}ε\right)$$ queries, where $u_k=\max_{2\leq j\leq k} \frac{(3k-j)2^{j-2}-k}{k(2^j-1)}$. The main ingredient is a recursive construction of collision states in which intermediate states are retained and reused. We bound both the preparation and reflection costs, and choose the number of retained states at each order so that all recursive steps fit within a common query budget. We combine this construction with a sequence of decreasing probability thresholds, estimating the contribution of the labels found along the way separately from the remaining power sum. A batch estimator gives the required bound in the high-precision regime, and an initial search removes the need for prior knowledge of the power sum.

</details>

Abstract: Flat bands support compact localized states (CLSs), whereas bound states in the continuum (BICs) remain spatially localized despite having energies embedded in the continuum of extended states. Abstract: In the present work we investigate how the stable and unstable periodic orbits of a perturbed two-dimensional oscillator influence the Bohmian trajectories of the corresponding quantum system. Abstract: Quantum communication provides a natural means to transmit quantum information, but how difficult is it to make such a transmission using only classical communication resources? We address this question by introducing classical communication models for quantum preparations and defining their minimum cost as the classical complexity. We prove that this complexity equals the amount of classical communication required to simulate quantum correlations in prepare-and-broadcast experiments, thus establishing a resource equivalence between quantum state transmission and quantum correlations. This calls for a general characterisation of the classical complexity of the noisy quantum state space, which we show grows exponentially with the quantum dimension and as a power-law in the inverse noise. It reveals the rapidly scaling fundamental cost of classical simulation. Moreover, we develop a toolbox of methods to certify classical complexity in quantum preparation devices, including an exact semidefinite-programming characterisation, analytical witnesses for symmetric preparations and convex programming scalable up to hundreds and sometimes thousands of qubit states. Our results establish classical complexity as a fundamental measure of quantum communication advantage, revealing the intrinsic cost of classically simulating quantum theory and providing practical tools for its certification.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上同一相内自由能解析且对称性自发破缺模式相同，人们倾向于认为不同温度的 Gibbs 态可相互转化。作者要澄清在"双向拟局域信道"这一资源受限的可转化性判据下，同一有序相内不同温度的态是否仍存在不可逆的资源序，从而给出超越自由能与对称破缺分类的温度层级不变量。

Abstract: Gibbs states at different temperatures can be inequivalent under two-way quasi-local channels even within a single phase with spontaneously broken continuous symmetry and analytic free energy. We prove this for the symmetry-invariant Gibbs states of a canonical ideal Bose gas at fixed density and a classical vortex-free Villain model on hypercubic lattices in $d\ge3$ dimensions. At lower temperatures, two distant observers can estimate the shared direction of order more accurately. The maximum mutual information they can obtain by separate measurements grows logarithmically with the radius of their regions. The leading term is the same throughout each ordered phase, while the constant term $\mathcal{C}(T)=\tfrac12\ln[Υ(T)/T]+c_{\mathrm{geom}}$ is strictly larger at lower temperatures. Here $Υ(T)$ is the phase stiffness and $c_{\mathrm{geom}}$ depends only on the region geometry. A quasi-local channel cannot increase this constant, so it is invariant under two-way conversion. Cooling would increase it and is therefore impossible between any two distinct temperatures in the ordered phase. On finite lattices, cooling with vanishing error requires a circuit whose causal radius grows at least linearly with the lattice side length. We also obtain the invariant for quantum and multicomponent Villain models and conjecture a stiffness formula for more general patterns of continuous symmetry breaking.

Motivation: Qubit丢失与泄漏是持续性的：只要某qubit处于丢失/泄漏状态，作用其上的所有操作都被丢弃，导致实际执行的Clifford电路逐shot变化。这一差异性破坏了Pauli-frame模拟的前提——它依赖在单一的“公共参考”测量记录上XOR采样得到的Pauli翻转来构造每个shot，因而需要新的理论框架来界定哪些容错原语仍可高效精确模拟。

Abstract: Qubit loss and leakage are persistent errors: while a qubit remains lost or leaked, every operation acting on it is dropped. This variation breaks Pauli-frame simulation, which constructs each shot by XORing sampled Pauli flips onto a single \emph{common reference} measurement record. Such circuits can therefore be simulated exactly under Pauli noise and loss by a Pauli frame with per-shot masks for the dropped operations. We define \emph{frame-CSS} circuits as those that are CSS up to local single-qubit basis changes, given a loss model that specifies where qubits can be lost and reloaded. We prove that every frame-CSS circuit has a common reference, and we give a practically near-linear-time procedure that certifies whether a circuit is frame-CSS. On the gross code under qubit loss and Pauli errors, \ellis{} samples 952{,}000 shots per second on a single CPU core, at least $1000\times$ faster than Clifft~\cite{chase2026} and PPVM~\cite{luo2026ppvm}.

</details>

Moreover, fully unitary synthesis does not exploit opportunities created by permitted output measurements and classical feedforward. We present CAVQS, a context-aware variational synthesis framework that combines unitary redistribution and dynamic reconstruction. First, CAVQS uses QR decomposition to transfer unitary factors between overlapping blocks, preserving the overall transformation while reshaping local synthesis targets to enable cross-block reductions. Second, for blocks that precede permitted terminal measurements, CAVQS constructs ancilla-free dynamic implementations with a CNOT-budgeted pre-measurement search and analytic branch-unitary construction, thereby minimizing expected CNOT cost. Across random-circuit targets, CAVQS reduces mean two-qubit gate counts by 28.79%-42.52%, 2.05%-52.62%, and 30.51%-74.64% relative to QFAST, SQUANDER, and Fast-USYN, respectively, on common successful instances. On 4--6-qubit quantum algorithm benchmarks, the corresponding reductions are 27.27%-36.77%, 20.65%-69.10%, and 36.31%-71.88%, respectively.

Abstract: The polynomial method is one of the fundamental techniques for proving lower bounds on quantum query complexity, particularly in the BQP query model. In this work, we develop an analogue of the polynomial method for the DQC1 query model. We introduce the notion of {\em sensitivity approximate degree} for Boolean functions and show that it provides a lower bound on DQC1 query complexity. We establish several basic structural properties of this new measure. As a first result, we show that every nonconstant total Boolean function on $N$ input variables has sensitivity approximate degree $Ω(N)$, and hence requires $Ω(N)$ queries in the DQC1 model. Thus, unlike in the BQP query model, DQC1 admits no asymptotic query speedup for total Boolean functions. For symmetric partial Boolean functions, we give a complete characterization of the sensitivity approximate degree in terms of the separation between oppositely labeled Hamming weight layers. This yields a tight quadratic separation between sensitivity approximate degree and standard approximate degree, and also leads to tight quadratic separations between DQC1 and BQP query complexities. We further establish quantitative average-case classical simulation theorems for DQC1 algorithms. Finally, we apply our framework to Fourier analysis, obtaining a simple proof of an $Ω(N^{1/4})$ DQC1 query lower bound for the 2-Forrelation problem.

</details>

Abstract: Accurate identification of the Hamiltonian governing a quantum system is important for quantum-system characterization, device calibration, simulation, and reliable operation of quantum hardware. In practical systems, however, the governing dynamics may vary over time because of parameter drift, changing control conditions, or transitions between operating regimes. The framework is evaluated on transverse-field Ising model systems under stationary dynamics, abrupt Hamiltonian switching, rapidly changing Hamiltonians, random switch locations over longer trajectories, and gradual Hamiltonian drift. We further compare the proposed approach with a Quantum Graph Recurrent Neural Network (QGRNN) baseline and evaluate frozen policies on unseen trajectories. Results show that both approaches accurately reconstruct stationary Hamiltonians, while GRU-QRL provides substantially improved tracking under non-stationary dynamics. Abstract: Photonic GHZ states generated with probabilistic pair sources can be described by edge-coloured, complex-weighted graphs. Summing over the colours at one fixed vertex reduces the problem to an \((N-1)\times N\) matrix of effective edge weights. For the graph amplitudes \(ω\) and GHZ target \(Δ\), the row--column imbalance yields \(\mathcal F(ω)=0\) but \(\mathcal F(Δ)\neq0\), contradicting the required equality \(ω=Δ\). Finally, we give a physical interpretation of the AI-discovered mathematical proof.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将哈密顿量分解为经典可处理片段是量子模拟的核心问题，语境子空间分解依赖泡利算符的对易/反对易关系，因此自然应把非语境性与费米子物理联系起来；但此前缺乏严格的形式化刻画，也未明确哪些片段对量子模拟真正有用。

Method: 以对易与反对易条件定义非语境泡利集合；证明两条核心定理：(1) 非语境泡利哈密顿量为自由费米子伪装(free-fermion-disguised)；(2) 可写成马约拉纳与狄拉克费米子算符形式，具有类Jordan–Wigner的费米子-量子比特编码；两定理用Lean证明助手形式化。基于此刻画费米子非语境哈密顿量空间，并提出寻找线图(line-graph)自由费米子片段及其连通分量的启发式算法。

Result: 形式化了非语境泡利哈密顿量与自由费米子伪装哈密顿量之间的关系并界定其空间结构；启发式算法在编码后的分子哈密顿量上与非语境搜索基准相比，在识别自由费米子可解分量方面性能相当。

Conclusion: 非语境泡利哈密顿量本质上是（伪装的）自由费米子模型，可作为可经典求解的片段；线图方法能高效提供此类片段，为量子模拟中片段选择提供理论依据与实用工具。

Abstract: Decomposing Hamiltonians into classically tractable fragments is central to quantum simulation. One such way of doing so is the contextual subspace decomposition, which expresses the Hamiltonian with respect to non-contextual sets of Paulis. The conditions for contextuality rely on the anticommutation and commutation of Pauli operators; it is therefore natural to make an explicit connection between non-contextuality and fermionic physics. Theoretical work is centered around two theorems: in the first, we consider non-contextual Pauli Hamiltonians and show that they are free-fermion-disguised. In the second we write these Pauli non-contextual Hamiltonians in terms of Majorana and Dirac fermionic operators, showing that they carry a Jordan--Wigner-like fermion-to-qubit encoding. These two central theorems are formalized in the proof assistant language Lean. We use this to describe the space of fermionic non-contextual Hamiltonians, and highlight their relation to the more general class of free-fermion-disguised Hamiltonians. We further develop a heuristic algorithm to find line-graph based free-fermion fragments and their connected components of molecular Hamiltonians. We benchmark our algorithms against non-contextual searches for encoded molecular Hamiltonians and find comparable performance in identifying free-fermion solvable components. Our work has implications for what fragments to consider as useful for quantum simulation.

</details>


### [71] [Stitching Quantum Codes into Quilts: Universal Logic with no Ancillary Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26329)
*Stasiu Wolanski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出"code quilt"构造，可在不使用辅助码块的情况下直接测量两个或多个码块之间的联合逻辑Pauli算符，并给出显式电路与充分条件，在双变量自行车码与表面码上验证了距离保持与实际纠错性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越表面码的量子码之间进行容错逻辑Pauli乘积测量已有诸多方案，但通常依赖辅助系统（ancilla blocks）。本文旨在消除对额外辅助系统的依赖，仅借助码块空闲综合提取已有的物理辅助比特，直接实现联合逻辑测量，从而降低开销并提升对实际码族的适用性。

Method: 引入基于子系统码的"code quilt"构造，将目标联合逻辑算符直接在码块间测量；给出显式测量电路，不引入除空闲综合所需辅助比特之外的物理量子比特。进行结构化分析，给出若干保证码容量距离得以保持的充分条件。提出两种协议变体：变体一直接测量子系统码的gauge生成元，电路实现实用（物理量子比特度最多增加2），但在大距离极限下可证非容错；变体二渐近性质更优，但未在一般情形下证明容错。随后对双变量自行车码与表面码开展大规模经验分析，对25,000个随机选取的逻辑算符寻找保持码容量距离的quilt，优化稀疏、低拥塞的探测器基，并在子集上进行解码实验。

Result: 对25,000个随机选择的逻辑算符，为双变量自行车码与表面码找到了保持码容量距离的quilt，并为每个算符优化出稀疏且低拥塞的探测器基；在随机子集上估计得到保持距离的电路。解码实验表明，在144-qubit双变量自行车码与距离12旋转表面码上、物理错误率1.5×10⁻³至3×10⁻³范围内，逻辑错误概率与空闲存储相当。

Conclusion: code quilt为跨码块逻辑Pauli乘积测量提供了一种无需额外辅助系统的可行途径，其变体一具有高度实用的电路实现并在实践中展现出与空闲存储相当的逻辑错误率；变体二具备更优渐近性质。整体结果支持该构造作为超越表面码容错逻辑操作的有力候选。

Abstract: Proposals abound for performing fault-tolerant logical Pauli-product measurements between quantum codes beyond the surface code. We introduce a new construction, the code quilt, whereby a target joint logical operator of two or more codeblocks is directly measured without the use of an ancillary system. We construct explicit circuits to perform a logical measurement using the quilt construction, which also require no physical qubits beyond the ancillas already in place to perform idle syndrome extraction on the codeblocks in question. We give a detailed structural analysis of the construction, which is based on a subsystem code, and provide several sets of sufficient conditions under which the relevant code-capacity distance is preserved. We give two variants of the protocol. The first, where the gauge generators of the subsystem code are measured directly, is provably not fault tolerant in the large-distance limit but admits a particularly practical circuit implementation in which the physical qubit degree increases by at most two. The second has more favourable asymptotic properties, although we do not prove its fault tolerance in the general case. We then perform an extensive empirical analysis of the technique, in particular of the first variant, as applied to bivariate bicycle and surface codes, finding code-capacity-distance preserving quilts for measuring 25,000 randomly selected logical operators. We optimise to find sparse and decongested detector bases for each and, for a randomly selected subset, find circuits that we estimate to be distance preserving. Finally, we perform decoding experiments on a subset of these circuits and find that their logical error probability is comparable to that of idle memory for operations involving the 144-qubit bivariate bicycle code and distance-12 rotated surface code at physical error rates between $1.5\times 10^{-3}$ and $3\times 10^{-3}$.

</details>

*Pedro R. Dieguez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文引入"非实在性(irrealism)"度量解码可观测量的实在性缺失程度，并针对量子随机存取码(QRAC)给出：在互无偏基下逐维精确的非实在性前沿、非实在性超额恒等式、以及纠缠辅助情形下的实在性非局域性关系与认证判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对"解码可观测量必须离'具有实在要素'多远才能实现给定量子通信优势"这一问题，作者希望量化可观测量的实在性缺失（非实在性）与QRAC成功概率之间的定量权衡关系，并统一处理prepare-and-measure(PM)与纠缠辅助(EA)两类编码。

Method: 将非实在性定义为可观测量的未揭示测量扰动制备时，该制备到最近"未扰动制备"的相对熵，涵盖局域相干性及被未揭示测量破坏的关联。对互无偏基解码的PM码推导"非实在性前沿"（每成功概率下最小总非实在性），并证得超额非实在性恒等式；进而对解码器作最小化，分析经典值以上所需非实在性；对含量子接收方、可信解码器、宇称依赖发送设置的EA码，将前沿与所用情境的实在性非局域性联系。

Result: 任意维度下精确给出互无偏基PM码的非实在性前沿；超额非实在性恒等式表明解码算符的Gibbs态唯一达到该前沿。最小化解码器后，所需非实在性在经典值以下为零、以上为正；对量子比特，成功概率低于0.833时互无偏解码器并非最省。EA情形前沿超出PM前沿的量等于最优码所用情境的实在性非局域性；在秩一投影发送测量下，成功概率高于0.815即可认证共享态具有最小实在性非局域性。

Conclusion: 非实在性是刻画QRAC量子优势的统一资源量；其在PM与EA码中的前沿可精确确定并用Gibbs态实现，且与实在性非局域性存在严格定量关系，从而为基于成功概率认证共享态非局域性提供阈值判据。

Abstract: How far must decoding observables be from having elements of reality to achieve a given quantum advantage in a communication task? We answer this question for quantum random access codes. Here, an observable has an element of reality when its unrevealed measurement leaves the preparation undisturbed; its irrealism is the relative entropy to the closest such preparation. Because it comprises local coherence and the correlations destroyed by the unrevealed measurement, irrealism applies to prepare-and-measure (PM) and entanglement-assisted (EA) codes alike. For PM codes decoded in mutually unbiased bases, we obtain the irrealism frontier, the least total irrealism compatible with each success probability, exactly in every dimension; an excess-irrealism identity shows that Gibbs states of the decoding operator attain it uniquely. For PM codes minimized also over the decoders, the required irrealism vanishes up to the classical value and is positive beyond it; for qubits, mutually unbiased decoders are not the cheapest below a success probability of 0.833. For EA codes with a qubit receiver, trusted decoders, and parity-dependent sender settings, the frontier exceeds the PM one by the realism-based nonlocality of the contexts used by an optimal code, and with rank-one projective sender measurements a success probability above 0.815 certifies a minimum realism-based nonlocality of the shared state.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清纠缠传播与可读信息（记录）在环境中建立的时间与空间关系，并刻画相对相位这一量子信息核心要素的可及性及量子态恢复能力。

Method: 采用顺序相互作用量子比特链这一精确可解模型；分析约化密度矩阵非对角元所编码的主导局域记录及其Pauli测量可探测性（布居测量仅给出高阶信号）；通过联合横向关联刻画相对相位的可及性；推导由系统与可及环境片段恢复未知量子态的最优保真度。

Conclusion: 纠缠传播、可读记录的建立与量子可恢复性三者并不等价：环境内部相互作用可在系统约化态不变时重排信息可及性，说明量子达尔文主义式的冗余记录与恢复区域需结合具体测量与动力学来界定。

Abstract: We investigate entanglement, readable records and quantum recovery using an exactly solvable chain of sequentially interacting qubits. For weak interactions, entanglement reaches a later environment site before that site carries an equally strong local record. The leading local record is encoded in an off-diagonal element of the reduced density matrix and can be detected by a Pauli measurement, while a population measurement gives a higher-order signal. We further characterize the accessibility of the relative phase through joint transverse correlations and derive the optimal fidelity for recovering an unknown quantum state from the system and an accessible environment fragment. We show that interactions acting only within the environment can redistribute readable records and change the region required for quantum recovery without affecting the reduced state of the system.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Page-Wootters构造（时间即量子钟读数、全局态定态）启发，希望实验上探索"测量时刻作为量子自由度"的可观测效应。

Method: 用schedule qubit将驱动量子比特的两个Zeno测量序列置于相干叠加；以物理memory qubits实现电路，通过测量memory寄存器联合X基宇称区分相干与经典/退相位时序；并给出超导比特实现的门数与误差预算。

Result: 固定驱动强度下，两序列的干涉可见度仅依赖二者时间偏移，与中间测量次数无关；对某一schedule读出结果，Zeno趋势反转，与标准Zeno概率互补；联合X基宇称可区分相干时序与经典或退相位时序。

Conclusion: 这些预言可由标准电路描述直接得出，无需关系性时间诠释；与Page-Wootters框架通过时钟-系统关联恢复标准量子动力学相一致。

Abstract: Motivated by the Page-Wootters construction in which time is a quantum clock reading and the global system state is stationary, we propose a quantum circuit that explores the observable consequences of treating measurement timing as a quantum degree of freedom. We place two Zeno measurement schedules of a driven qubit in a coherent superposition using a schedule qubit. We show that, for a fixed drive strength, the interference visibility between the two schedules depends only on the time offset between them, not on the number of intermediate measurements taken. Then we show that, for one schedule-readout outcome, the Zeno trend reverses, predicting a result that complements the standard Zeno probability. We propose a circuit implementation with physical memory qubits and show that measuring the memory register's joint $X$-basis parity distinguishes a coherent schedule from a classical or dephased one. We give the gate count and an error budget for a superconducting-qubit implementation. The Page-Wootters picture motivates this protocol and provides an equivalent stationary representation, although the predictions follow from the standard circuit description without requiring a relational-time interpretation. This is consistent with the Page-Wootters framework recovering standard quantum dynamics through correlations between a clock and the rest of the system.

</details>

Conclusion: 非对称性可作为一种调控手段，用于加速集体自发辐射并提升两原子系统的纠缠生成效率，且该效应在有损耗腔中依然成立。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics and the spontaneous decay of two identical two-level atoms into the vacuum in a cavity at low temperature. We analytically derive the survival probability and the amplitudes of the state of the system by calculating the exact self-energy when the atoms are both initially excited. It is found that moderate asymmetry enhances the decay of the survival probability and increases the entanglement production in either lossless and lossy cavities.

</details>


### [76] [Efficient Classical Algorithms for Ground Energy Estimation of Fermions with Conjugate-Pair Orbital Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26382)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米子基态能量估计是量子多体物理核心问题。经典方法多依赖量子蒙特卡洛，符号问题是主要障碍；即便存在无符号问题的非负展开，快速混合的严格保证仍不清楚，且现有量子算法多需结构性假设或谱隙、弱耦合条件。

Method: 先将基态能量估计归约为配分函数估计；采用可控能量误差的离散化，将配分函数表示为行列式模平方的非负加权和；再应用Chen–Liu对数凹计数定理获得快速混合与可计算性保证；同时通过控制独立采样次数与矩阵更新次数、利用稀疏矩阵降低单次更新代价，得到更紧的算术复杂度界。
