### [93] [A Computational and Experimental Framework for Fourier Optics in Python](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25116)
*Rachel Migdal,Antoine Islegen-Wojdyla*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文介绍基于傅里叶光学的光传播仿真基础及其Python实现，重点讨论尺寸（量纲）问题，并通过解析公式对标、衍射光学元件生成与相位恢复实验衔接来验证仿真正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学系统设计与尺寸标定需要可靠的光传播仿真，而数值波光学仿真中物理空间缩放容易出错，缺乏系统的验证与实践指南。

Method: 采用傅里叶光学框架在Python中实现波传播数值仿真，强调空间尺寸与采样参数的正确处理；先验证数值仿真保持正确的物理空间缩放，再将数值传播结果与高斯光束、透镜聚焦、光栅衍射的闭式解析公式做基准比对；进而利用该仿真生成菲涅尔波带片等衍射光学元件，并通过采集实验数据结合相位恢复算法恢复相位信息。

Result: 数值波光学仿真在正确尺寸标定下与高斯光束、透镜聚焦及光栅衍射的解析解吻合；仿真可生成菲涅尔波带片类衍射光学元件；相位恢复算法可从实验数据中重建相位，实现数值与实验的衔接。

Conclusion: 基于傅里叶光学的Python传播仿真在保证尺寸正确性时是可靠且实用的工具，可用于光学系统设计、衍射元件生成与实验相位测量。

Abstract: Simulation of the propagation of light is useful for the design and dimensioning of optical systems. In this paper, we present the basics of propagation simulation using Fourier optics and practical implementation in Python, with a particular emphasis on dimensions. We first verify that our numerical wave-optics simulations maintain correct physical spatial scaling. Next, we benchmark numerical propagation against closed-form analytical formulas for Gaussian beams, lens focusing effects, and grating diffraction. We then show how those simulations can be used to generate diffractive optical elements such as Fresnel zone plates, and we bridge the gap between numerical simulations and experiments by collecting data and recovering phase information using a phase retrieval algorithm.

</details>


### [94] [Nodal Knotted Finite-Energy Spatiotemporally Localized Scalar Wave Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25155)
*Ioannis M. Besieris,Peeter Saari*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 文章填补了标量型时空局域波中缺乏节线纽结拓扑解的空白，推导出具有Hopf结与三叶结节线拓扑的有限能量标量脉冲解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单色傍轴光束的节线纽结（零强度暗线的纽结）已有大量研究，矢量型有限能量时空局域波也已知具有纽结拓扑；但除描述反常色散的类薛定谔方程解之外，尚缺乏具有类似纽结拓扑结构的标量型时空局域波解。

Method: 推导有限能量的标量型时空局域波解，使其节线（零强度线）分别呈现Hopf结与三叶结拓扑结构。

Result: 获得具有Hopf结和三叶结节线拓扑的有限能量标量时空局域脉冲波解。

Conclusion: 该工作将具有非平凡拓扑结构的单色光束在粒子俘获与引导、图像处理、光通信以及生物医学中的应用，推广到时空局域脉冲场情形。

Abstract: Significant amount of information is available in wave optics about monochromatic paraxial beams characterized by nodal knots, the latter being threads of complete darkness (lines of zero intensity) tied into knots. Also, well -known are finite-energy spatiotemporally localized vector-valued wave solutions characterized by knotted to-pologies. Unfortunately, except for solutions to the Schrödinger-like equation describing anomalous dispersion, no scalar-valued spatiotemporally localized waves with analogous knotted topological structures are known to date. It is the goal in this article to fill in this gap by deriving finite-energy spatiotemporally localized scalar-valued wave solutions characterized specifically by Hopf and trefoil nodal knot topologies. This will extend to spatiotemporally localized pulsed fields the diverse important applications of mono-chromatic light beams, having nontrivial topological structures, in trapping and guiding particles, image processing and optical communications; also, in applications to biology and medicine.

</details>


### [95] [A universal modal-noise limit for multimode photonic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25156)
*Kalaga Madhav*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 多模到单模光子系统的模态噪声不受器件差异影响，在固定输入功率和各向同性模式激发条件下，任意线性多模系统的相对功率涨落由单一数值——K=T†WT特征值的参与比M_eff决定；该规律经蒙特卡洛验证并给出光谱相关与速度误差的闭式表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模态噪声限制所有多模到单模光子系统的光度与光谱稳定性，但现有研究逐个器件单独量化，缺乏统一的、器件无关的描述框架。

Method: 在各向同性模式激发与固定输入功率条件下，以K=T†WT的特征值参与比M_eff作为刻画系统分数功率涨落的单一参数；对空间滤波光纤、损耗型lantern和集成模式转换器进行蒙特卡洛模拟验证；进而将K推广为K(λ)以推导模态噪声的光谱相关性与速度误差闭式解。

Result: 蒙特卡洛结果确认该规律成立，并能退化给出经典散斑极限；K(λ)给出模态噪声的光谱相关性和速度误差闭式表达，且当相关宽度等于线宽时误差最大。

Conclusion: 任意线性多模光子系统的模态噪声可由M_eff这一单参数普适刻画，提供器件无关的量化框架，并可推广至光谱相关与光谱仪速度误差分析。

Abstract: Modal noise limits the photometric and spectroscopic stability of every multimode-to-single-mode photonic system, yet it is quantified device by device. For isotropic modal excitation at fixed input power, the fractional power fluctuation of any linear multimode system is shown to be fixed by one number: the participation ratio M_eff of the eigenvalues of K = T†WT. Monte Carlo for spatially filtered fibres, lossy lanterns, and integrated mode converters confirms the law, which recovers the classical speckle limits. Extending K to K(λ) gives the spectral correlation of modal noise and a closed-form velocity error, maximal when the correlation width equals the line width.

</details>


### [96] [The Complete Set of Solutions for the Equations of Temporal Coupled Mode Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25181)
*Matthew Beutel,Ming Zhou,Shanhui Fan*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文推导了时间耦合模理论（TCMT）在半无耗单模/多模谐振腔耦合任意端口数情形下、受时间反演对称性、互易性与能量守恒约束的全部通解，方法基于对称酉背景散射矩阵的显式Takagi分解，并与数值模拟对比验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TCMT用少量物理透明的参数（耦合系数与直接背景散射矩阵）紧凑描述光学谐振输运，其背景散射矩阵与耦合矢量受时间反演对称性、互易性与能量守恒约束。这些约束虽已确立，但其通解仅在借助空间对称性简化的少数情形下已知，缺乏一般性解。

Method: 对描述无耗单模或多模谐振腔耦合任意数量端口的TCMT方程，利用对称酉背景散射矩阵的显式Takagi分解，推导出满足上述约束的完整解集。

Result: 给出了单模与多模情形下的完整通解，并通过与数值模拟的对比验证了所推导解的正确性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于Takagi分解的框架无须依赖空间对称性即可给出TCMT约束方程的一般解，扩展了TCMT的适用范围。

Abstract: Temporal coupled mode theory (TCMT) provides a compact description of resonant transport in optical systems in terms of a small set of physically transparent parameters, including the resonant coupling coefficients and direct background scattering matrix. In the formalism of TCMT, the background scattering matrix and the coupling vectors are constrained by time reversal symmetry, reciprocity, and energy conservation. While these constraints are well established, the general so- lution under these is only known for a few cases, in which spatial symmetry simplifies the problem. In this paper, we derive the complete set of solutions for the equations of temporal coupled mode theory that describes lossless single-mode or multi-mode resonators coupled to an arbitrary num- ber of ports. Our derivation utilizes an explicit Takagi decomposition of the symmetric unitary background scattering matrix. We provide a comparison with numerical simulations for both the single and multimode cases.

</details>


### [97] [High-Dimensional Structured Light Tomography with a Tilted Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25427)
*Altilano C. Barbosa,Isaac Tribaldo,Amanda K. Fritsch,Marcos G. de Oliveira,Gabriel Molina-Terriza,Antonio Z. Khoury*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 利用倾斜透镜实现像散变换与强度测量，将空间模式层析推广至非固定阶数，维度达D=66且保真度良好，并系统修正了倾斜透镜传播理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前方法的空间模式层析受限于固定阶数，无法覆盖更高维模式空间；同时文献对倾斜透镜中光传播的描述不一致，缺乏准确变换模型。

Method: 基于强度测量与倾斜透镜产生的像散变换进行高维空间模式层析，改进Ref.[1]方法以解除阶数限制；推导倾斜透镜的有效焦距，分析其作为像散模式转换器的作用，并在厄米-高斯基下确定其显式适用范围。

Result: 实现了非固定阶数的空间层析，在模式空间维度D=66时仍获得良好保真度；给出倾斜透镜有效焦距及作为像散模式转换器的完整理论描述与适用域。

Conclusion: 倾斜透镜像散变换可用于高维空间模式层析，且不受固定阶数限制；所建立的理论框架为该方法提供了准确、自洽的物理基础。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate high-dimensional spatial mode tomography using intensity measurements and the astigmatic transformation implemented with a tilted lens. We improve the method previously published in Ref. [1] to perform the spatial tomography not restricted to a fixed order, which allowed us to achieve good fidelity for a mode space dimension as high as $D=66\,$. Since the literature is not unanimous, we also present a comprehensive revision of light propagation through a tilted lens for an accurate description of the transformation used for tomography. We derive its effective focal lengths, and analyze its role as an astigmatic mode converter. Moreover, we establish its explicit domain of validity within the Hermite-Gaussian basis.

</details>


### [98] [A fast and accurate model for gradient-based inverse design of multimode waveguides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25432)
*Yuanze Chen,Zheng-Hua Chen,Yan-Xiao Gong,Shi-Ning Zhu*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出基于耦合模理论（CMT）的可微代理模型，通过模态基变换提取耦合模参数，用于多模光子器件的快速、准确逆设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模光子逆设计需要快速、准确且能提供几何梯度的计算模型，现有方法（如基于前向数据集的EME）在跨越模耦合区时预测精度不足。

Method: 基于CMT构建可微代理模型；采用模态基变换从截面模场数据中提取耦合模参数；给出完整、易实现的传播与优化流程；以FDTD为公共参考，对随机生成的薄膜铌酸锂（TFLN）波导轮廓（跨越模耦合区）对比本模型与基于前向数据集的本征模展开（EME）的预测精度。

Result: 在相同模态库下，本模型相比前向EME将单模平均透射率绝对误差最大值降低17–37%；设计出在与一阶TE₁模杂化区保持基模TM₀的模斑转换器，获得9个结构，FDTD验证透射率均高于98%。

Conclusion: 该模型兼顾梯度信息与更高预测精度，适用于大规模光子逆设计与设计参数空间的快速探索。

Abstract: Multimode photonic inverse design requires fast, accurate computational models that provide geometry gradients. Based on coupled-mode theory (CMT), we propose a differentiable surrogate model. Its parameter-extraction method uses a change of modal basis to obtain coupled-mode parameters from cross-sectional mode data. We also provide a complete, easy-to-implement propagation and optimization workflow. Using finite-difference time-domain (FDTD) simulation data as a common reference, we compare the prediction accuracy of our model and forward dataset-based eigenmode expansion (EME) for randomly generated thin-film lithium niobate (TFLN) waveguide profiles that traverse modal coupling regions. Using identical modal libraries, the model reduces the maximum per-mode mean absolute transmission error by 17-37% compared with forward EME. We demonstrate a spot-size converter design that preserves the fundamental TM$_0$ mode in its hybridization region with the first-order TE$_1$ mode and obtain nine structures, each with FDTD-verified transmission above 98%. These tests demonstrate the model's utility for large-scale photonic inverse design, enabling rapid exploration of the design parameter space by combining gradient information with improved prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [99] [High-fidelity six-dimensional optical data storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25545)
*Jichao Gao,Tong Jin,Chenqi Yi,Yihui Fu,Zhi Yan,Xiya Xie,Rokas Drevinskas,Zhexiang Xiao,Lei Wang,Guangda Niu,Jingyu Zhang,Hong-Bo Sun*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出一种六维（6D）偏振光复用方法，利用三维介质中的激光诱导纳米结构实现高保真多维光存储，原始准确率达99.45%，单盘有效用户容量达0.38 Pb。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多维光存储通过单个体素编码多位信息可大幅提升存储密度，但编码维度增多会加剧维度间串扰，损害记录保真度，因此需要在提高维度的同时抑制串扰。

Method: 利用偏振光与三维介质的超快相互作用，在类庞加莱半球上操控三个光参数，使其系统性地激发激光诱导纳米结构中的三种材料响应，分别对应荧光、相位延迟和慢轴取向这三个物理量，形成光参数与材料响应之间的一一对应关系。

Result: 凭借光参数与材料响应的多重一一对应，实现前所未有的高保真记录，原始准确率99.45%；独立控制的6D复用技术将有效用户容量提升至每盘0.38 Pb。

Conclusion: 激光诱导纳米结构在多个物理维度上的可调控性可推广至微流控、光学元件、集成光子学、微机械及量子技术等多种应用。

Abstract: Multi-dimensional optical data storage, wherein each voxel encodes multiple bits of information within a three-dimensional isotropic medium, presents a revolutionary approach to dense data storage. This approach exploits multiple degrees of freedom of light, such as intensity, polarization and wavelength, to selectively control distinct optical responses in the medium. However, increasing dimensionality exacerbates crosstalk between different encoding dimensions, thereby compromising recording fidelity. In this study, we introduce a novel six-dimensional (6D) multiplexing approach that exploits ultrafast interactions of polarized light with a three-dimensional medium. By manipulating three light parameters within a Poincaré-like semi-sphere, we systematically induce three distinct material responses in laser-induced nanostructures, each corresponding to a unique physical quantity: fluorescence, phase retardance, and slow axis orientation. Thanks to such multiple one-to-one correspondence between light parameters and material responses, we achieve unprecedented high fidelity, attaining a raw accuracy of 99.45%. Furthermore, this independently controlled 6D multiplexing technique maximizes the effective user capacity to 0.38 petabits per disc. The versatility of laser-induced nanostructures across multiple physical dimensions enables diverse applications, ranging from high-capacity data storage to microfluidics, optical elements, integrated photonics, micromechanics, and quantum technologies.

</details>


### [100] [Direct measurement of the on-shot focal-spot size of a kilojoule-class petawatt laser](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25552)
*Ryunosuke Takizawa,Keisuke Takahashi,Yuhei Kawakami,Hiroki Matsubara,Yuga Karaki,Rinya Akematsu,Ryou Oomura,Kai Kimura,Yuki Tamaru,Yasunobu Arikawa,Koji Tsubakimoto,Shinsuke Fujioka*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 报道了千焦级四束拍瓦激光焦斑尺寸的直接单发测量：通过铝球法推断环围能量曲线，贝叶斯拟合双高斯混合得D50；最优准直束D50=34(+4/-5)μm（3倍衍射极限），满功率下热畸变使D50增至102(+9/-8)μm，峰值强度降至2.2(+0.6/-0.9)×10^18 W/cm^2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 千焦级拍瓦激光的焦斑尺寸决定峰值强度，是用户实验的关键参数，但满功率、单发条件下的直接测量困难；需要为设施用户提供可信的参考焦斑数值，并评估热畸变与自适应光学的实际影响。

Method: 将不同直径的铝涂层球置于焦平面，由球周围透射能量推断环围能量曲线；对每束光用贝叶斯推断拟合双分量高斯混合模型求取50%环围能量直径D50；在所有变形镜闭环优化条件下测量准直束，并在满功率（250 J、1.3 ps）条件下对比；另在脉冲压缩器上游加装变形镜（目前仅一束）以提升单发峰值强度。

Result: 最优校正准直束D50=34(+4/-5)μm，为10 μm衍射极限的约3倍；满功率下主放大器热畸变使D50展宽至102(+9/-8)μm，峰值强度下降约9倍至2.2(+0.6/-0.9)×10^18 W/cm^2；窄分量能量占比由68%降至38%。压缩器上游变形镜使单发峰值强度提升约一倍。

Conclusion: 单发铝球法结合贝叶斯双高斯拟合可可靠给出拍瓦激光焦斑D50，所获数值可作为设施用户的参考基准；主放大器热畸变是满功率焦斑展宽与峰值强度损失的主要来源，压缩器前变形镜可有效恢复峰值强度。

Abstract: We report a direct, on-shot determination of the focal-spot size of a kilojoule-class, four-beam petawatt laser. Encircled-energy curves were inferred from the energy transmitted around aluminum-coated spheres of varying diameters placed at the focal plane. The 50% encircled-energy diameter D50 of each beam was obtained by fitting a two-component Gaussian mixture with Bayesian inference. With all deformable mirrors optimized in closed loop, the best-corrected alignment beam reached D50 = 34 (+4/-5) um, three times the diffraction limit of 10 um. Under full-power conditions, the thermal distortion of the main amplifier broadened this beam to D50 = 102 (+9/-8) um and reduced its peak intensity ninefold, to 2.2 (+0.6/-0.9) x 10^18 W/cm^2 at 250 J and 1.3 ps. The energy fraction of the narrow component decreased from 68% to 38%. These reference values serve facility users, and a deformable mirror upstream of the pulse compressor, now on one beam only, doubles the on-shot peak intensity.

</details>


### [101] [Seamless Gradient Pixels for Multidimensional Optical Information Interrogation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25598)
*Zhenchao Liu,Bochang Wu,Houxin Fan,Tingbiao Guo,Hongtao Wang,Lei Chen,Cheng-Feng Pan,Shutao Zhang,Md Abdur Rahman,Wen Siang Lew,Hongyoon Kim,Heechang Yun,Junsuk Rho,Joel K. W. Yang*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出"梯度像素"超表面平台，通过设计参数在二维上连续变化，将多维光场信息编码为可测空间图案，实现单次快照的光谱-偏振同步探测及近场折射率传感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光与环境的相互作用蕴含丰富信息，但传统系统依赖分立元件和多探测通道，难以在单一紧凑平台上同时捕获并解译多维光场信息。

Method: 构建"梯度像素"超表面：在二维方向连续变化同一设计参数（如蛇形/serpentine构型），把光谱与偏振信息编码进空间图案；以自由曲面梯度像素实现定制化波长选择性图案；相邻meta-atom无缝拼接以提高通道空间密度并放宽Q因子约束；通过参数空间映射的显式读出，将远场探测与近场探测统一到同一图像读出框架。

Result: 在250 μm占位面积上，于100 nm光谱跨度内实现优于100 pm的光谱分辨率；快照式波长-偏振重构的偏振角精度达0.3°；并实现视频速率下的近场折射率传感。

Conclusion: 该工作将远场探测与近场传感通过统一的图像式读出相连接，可实现光场与局域介电环境的空间分辨分析，为多维光学传感、计算成像与智能信息处理提供新途径。

Abstract: Information from light interaction with its environment can be extracted from its numerous properties. Yet capturing and interpreting this information within a single and compact platform remains challenging because conventional systems rely on discrete components and detection channels. Here we introduce the concept of "gradient pixels" that provides a universal metasurface platform to interrogate optical field information in the infrared (IR) spectrum. By continuously varying a design parameter across two dimensions, we encode multidimensional optical information into measurable spatial patterns, e.g., both spectral and polarization information are acquired from a single snapshot image. A free-form gradient pixel further enables prescribed wavelength-selective spatial patterns within a single metasurface through tailored gradient profiles. Unlike isolated filter arrays, seamless gradient-pixels multiplex neighbouring meta-atoms, increasing channel spatial density and relaxing Q-factor constraints. A 250-$μ$m footprint of these meta-atoms achieves spectral resolution better than 100-pm over a 100-nm spectral span based on a serpentine configuration. Snapshot wavelength-polarization reconstruction yields polarization angular accuracy at 0.3$^\circ$. We extend this concept to perform near-field refractive index sensing at video rates using the same explicit parameter-space mapping. By linking far-field detection and near-field sensing, it enables spatially resolved analysis of optical fields and local dielectric environments through a common image-based readout. It offers prospects for multidimensional optical sensing, computational imaging and intelligent information processing.

</details>


### [102] [Real-Time Single-Pixel Imaging via Exploiting Temporal Mixing: A Unified Calibration and Composite Readout Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25700)
*Jing-Sen Li,En-Rui Zhang,Kan-Xu Jia,Yi-Zhu Zhang,Qian-Qian Bao,Shao-Ying Meng,Xi-Hao Chen*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文提出可迁移的统一标定与复合读出框架，将单像素成像中调制与电光采集链失配引起的时间记忆从"读出限制"转化为"测量资源"，实现高保真、低成本、低带宽的高帧率重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速光学测量与计算成像（如单像素成像）中，调制与电光采集链之间的失配导致时间混叠，传统上被视为破坏测量正交性、破坏压缩感知先验、限制成像速度并引发重建灾难性失败的无解失真。

Method: 建立统一的标定（calibration）与复合读出（composite-readout）框架：对采集链的时间记忆进行建模与标定，直接由混合观测重建目标场景，无需先恢复各个编码图案的单独投影；并保证标定流程可跨硬件平台迁移。

Result: 实现稳健的高保真恢复；在多种工况下，低带宽、低成本配置可稳定获得优于更高带宽参考系统的成像质量；支持高速与视频帧率单像素成像；基于数字微镜器件（DMD）建立的标定流程可迁移至旋转编码盘等其他光学系统。

Conclusion: 将标定的时间记忆确立为一种通用、可迁移的测量范式，把历史性硬件瓶颈转化为主动成像优势，为更广泛的计算成像提供新思路。

Abstract: In high-speed optical measurements and computational imaging such as in single-pixel imaging temporal mixing arising from the mismatch between modulation and the electro-optical acquisition chain is conventionally treated as an unmitigated distortion that corrupts measurement orthogonality, breaks compressive sensing priors, fundamentally limits imaging speed, and induces catastrophic reconstruction failure. Here, we establish a transferable, unified calibration and composite-readout framework for single-pixel imaging that systematically repurposes temporal memory from a readout limitation into a powerful measurement resource. Beyond achieving robust high-fidelity recovery, this engineered approach enables cost effective, lower bandwidth configurations to consistently deliver superior image quality compared to higher bandwidth reference setups across diverse operating conditions turning a historical hardware bottleneck into an active imaging advantage. Specifically, target scenes are reconstructed directly from mixed observations without the need to recover individual pattern projections, thereby significantly advancing high-speed and video-rate single-pixel imaging capabilities. Crucially, the transferability of our framework allows calibration routines established on one hardware platform (such as digital micromirror device-based setups) to be readily transferred across different optical systems (such as rotating coding disks), paving the way for calibrated temporal memory as a versatile and generalized measurement paradigm for broader computational imaging.

</details>


### [103] [Probing thin films and their interfaces via angle-resolved second-harmonic generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25746)
*J. Lukes,D. S. Gaur,V. Kanclir,K. Zidek*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出结合角度-偏振分辨SHG测量与含多重反射和光干涉的理论模型，将二次谐波响应分解为表面、体相与埋入界面三部分贡献，并用合成数据评估分解的唯一性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学薄膜性能与可靠性要求提高，需要更灵敏、无损的表征手段来研究薄膜界面与局部机械应力；SHG可追踪薄膜表面、体相及埋入界面的局部对称性变化，但三区域贡献难以区分。

Method: 建立考虑薄层内多重反射与光干涉的SHG理论模型；开展角度分辨与偏振分辨测量；先在沉积于不同玻璃衬底的Ta2O5薄膜上实验验证拟合流程；再生成合成数据集检验分解的唯一性与准确性，并考察膜厚、测量噪声、线性光学参数不确定度及所用偏振通道对非线性极化率反演的影响。

Result: 拟合能准确重现实测SHG响应，揭示埋入界面贡献存在显著的衬底依赖性，而表面与有效体相响应基本一致；合成数据检验表明，可靠分离各SHG贡献需准确的线性光学参数、多偏振通道的全局拟合以及足够显著的干涉特征。

Conclusion: SHG可用于光学薄膜的区域特异性表征，但可靠性受线性光学参数精度、偏振通道选择与干涉特征强弱制约，明确了该方法的能力边界与实际限制。

Abstract: Increasing demands on the performance and reliability of optical thin films stimulate the development of more sensitive characterisation methods capable of investigating thin-film interfaces or local mechanical stress. Second-harmonic generation (SHG) is a phenomenon that can serve as a non-destructive probe, tracing local symmetry changes in the film surface, bulk, and buried interfaces. Here, we investigate the decomposition of the SHG response from these three regions by combining angle- and polarisation-resolved measurements. We utilised a theoretical model of SHG accounting for multiple reflections and optical interference in a thin layer. The theory was first experimentally evaluated using Ta2O5 films deposited on different glass substrates. The fitting procedure accurately reproduced the measured SHG responses, revealing pronounced substrate-dependent variations in the buried-interface contribution, while the surface and effective-bulk responses remained similar. As the next step, we studied the reliability of the SHG signal decomposition into the surface, bulk and buried-interface components. The decomposition was evaluated by generating synthetic datasets and testing whether their retrieval was unique and accurate. The influence of film thickness, measurement noise, uncertainties in linear-optical parameters, and the polarisation channels used on the retrieval of nonlinear susceptibilities was also examined. Our results demonstrate that reliable separation of SHG contributions requires accurate linear optical parameters, global fitting of multiple polarisation channels, and sufficiently pronounced interference features, outlining the capabilities and practical limitations of SHG-based region-specific characterisation of optical thin films.

</details>


### [104] [Sign-Controlled BIC Merging and Flatband Formation in C2-symmetric Photonic Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25805)
*Jiayao Liu,Zhuoyang Li,Zimeng Zeng,Jinpeng Wang,Zelong He,Zhaona Wang*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文建立四模非厄米哈密顿量，阐明连续域束缚态(BIC)与平带的相互作用，揭示BIC合并、高阶辐射抑制与带平坦化各自依赖的独立条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平带上的BIC有望实现长寿命、弱色散光子，但二者的协同机制尚不清楚，尤其是BIC能否与平带共存或合并缺少理论判据。

Method: 针对C2对称光子晶体板构建四模非厄米哈密顿量，分析组间耦合符号对两个Friedrich-Wintgen(FW) BIC与一个对称保护(SP) BIC能否落入同一光子带的影响；在矩形晶格中推导辐射损耗的完全平方形式，并研究偏离矩形几何（非共线辐射偏振）对Q因子的作用。

Result: 组间耦合符号决定BIC合并能否发生（合并的必要条件）；矩形晶格中合并消除二次辐射损耗项、强烈抑制四次项，给出Q~k^-6；偏离矩形几何使FW-BIC转为准BIC，Q~ (90°-θ)^-2并恢复渐近Q~k^-2；二次带平坦化对耦合符号不敏感，可在无BIC合并甚至在斜晶格中发生。

Conclusion: BIC合并、高阶辐射抑制与带平坦化受不同条件支配：前者依赖耦合符号与矩形几何，后者独立于BIC合并，从而为平带BIC器件的设计提供分离可控的自由度。

Abstract: Bound states in the continuum (BICs) on flatbands promise long-lived, weakly dispersive photons, yet their interplay remains elusive. Here, we develop a four-mode non-Hermitian Hamiltonian for $C_2$-symmetric photonic-crystal slabs. We show that the sign of the interpair coupling determines whether two Friedrich-Wintgen (FW) BICs and a symmetry-protected (SP) BIC occupy the same photonic band, a necessary condition for merging. In rectangular lattices, merging eliminates the quadratic radiative-loss term in a perfect-square form and strongly suppresses the quartic contribution, giving an effective $Q \sim k^{-6}$ scaling. Deviations from rectangular geometry induce noncollinear radiation polarizations, converting FW-BICs into quasi-BICs with $Q_{\mathrm{qBIC}} \sim (90^\circ-θ)^{-2}$ and restoring the asymptotic $Q \sim k^{-2}$ scaling. Unlike BIC band selection, quadratic band flattening is insensitive to the coupling sign and can occur without BIC merging, even in oblique lattices. Our results reveal distinct conditions governing BIC merging, higher-order radiation suppression, and band flattening.

</details>


### [105] [Photonic processors that carry their own unclonable fingerprint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26079)
*Oxel Arnal Martínez,Pablo Martínez-Carrasco Romero,David Álvarez Outerelo,Francisco Javier Fraile-Peláez,José Capmany Francoy*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 通过各向同性环形路由重新分配六边形网格中光的路由，抑制距离相关功率衰减并提升响应熵，将可编程光子处理器改造为高容量可重构硬件信任根。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可编程光子处理器天然携带大量可调干涉仪制造缺陷，被视为物理不可克隆函数（PUF）的理想候选；但网格拓扑本身会把光汇聚到短路径上，使大部分无序对响应贡献甚微，限制了熵与容量。

Method: 提出各向同性环形路由方案：将光引导至六边形网格核心，再均匀分配到周边；仅通过重编程即可实现。结合光谱读出，在两片独立制造的72单元硅光处理器上进行验证，并考察被动相位完全补偿后的响应复现能力。

Result: 抑制14.7 dB的距离相关功率衰减，响应平均香农熵由1.95提升至3.48 bits；单片每次挑战输出1107 bits，比既有结果高两个数量级；挑战间与芯片间汉明距离分别为49.8%和50.0%，片内距离4.9%；名义相同的器件即使补偿被动相位仍无法复现目标响应。

Conclusion: 各向同性路由使通用可编程光子处理器成为可重构、高容量的硬件信任根，兼具PUF所需的唯一性与可靠性。

Abstract: Physically unclonable functions (PUFs) derive cryptographic keys from uncontrollable fabrication disorder, removing the need to store secrets in vulnerable non-volatile memory. Programmable photonic processors are natural candidates for PUFs, because each of their many tunable interferometers carries its own fabrication imperfections. However, we show that the mesh topology itself funnels light along short paths, so that most of this disorder contributes little to the response. Here we introduce an isotropic ring routing scheme that steers light to the core of a hexagonal mesh and redistributes it uniformly towards the perimeter, suppressing a distance-dependent power decay of 14.7 dB and raising the mean Shannon entropy of the response from 1.95 to 3.48 bits by reprogramming alone. Experiments on two independently fabricated 72-unit silicon processors show that a nominally identical device cannot reproduce the target's responses even after its passive phase profile is fully compensated. Combining isotropic routing with spectral readout yields 1107 bits per challenge, exceeding previous results by two orders of magnitude, with 49.8% inter-challenge and 50.0% inter-chip Hamming distances and a 4.9% intra-chip distance. These results establish general-purpose programmable photonic processors as reconfigurable, high-capacity hardware roots of trust.

</details>


### [106] [High-Speed Widefield Hyperspectral Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26097)
*Joana M. Teixeira,Tomás Lopes,Tiago D. Ferreira,Catarina S. Monteiro,Pedro A. S. Jorge,Nuno A. Silva*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出基于事件相机的傅里叶变换高光谱显微术：将神经形态事件相机与共路双折射干涉仪（连续扫描）耦合，通过两种互补采集策略（稳态宽带照明下的导数型干涉图事件流、LED时间调制下的强度型事件响应）实现超高速宽场高光谱成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统傅里叶变换高光谱成像需在光程扫描过程中采集密集的全场干涉图序列，数据量大、吞吐量低（步进扫描参考配置约需15分钟），限制了其速度与实时应用。

Method: 采用神经形态事件相机与共路双折射干涉仪结合的连续扫描架构，研究两种采集策略：(1) 恒定宽带照明，事件流对应导数形式的干涉图；(2) 对宽带LED施加时间调制，产生类强度的事件响应。随后从事件数据重建光谱立方体，并与基于帧和光谱仪的参考结果对比；进一步开展反射模式成像及运动粒子的运动补偿重建。

Result: 两种方法均能复原滤光片的主要光谱轮廓，与帧式和光谱仪参考定性吻合；相比步进扫描约15分钟的采集时间，加速超过3个数量级。导数法约200 ms获取有效干涉范围，等效速率约为5个高光谱数据立方体/秒；调制辅助法实验测得光谱分辨率17.9 nm，有效采集速率2.5个数据立方体/秒。反射成像与运动补偿重建验证了超越静态透射测量的空间分辨光谱恢复能力。

Conclusion: 事件相机与共路双折射干涉仪的结合可在连续扫描下实现实时、宽场高光谱成像，具备高速、高吞吐优势，并可扩展到反射模式与动态样品，是一种有前景的连续宽场高光谱成像技术。

Abstract: Fourier-transform hyperspectral imaging is an emerging technology that combines widefield acquisition with flexible spectral resolution. Yet, it typically requires the acquisition of dense sequences of full-field interferograms during optical-path scanning, which ultimately reduces the final throughput. To circumvent this limitation, this manuscript introduces an event-based Fourier-transform hyperspectral microscopy technique that exploits the coupling of a neuromorphic event camera with a common-path birefringent interferometer operated under continuous scanning. Specifically, two complementary acquisition strategies are investigated, one with steady broadband illumination where the event stream corresponds to a derivative-like interferogram, and another with a temporal modulation of a broadband LED, which produces an intensity-like event response. Both approaches are able to retrieve the principal spectral profiles of photographic filters, showing a good qualitative match with both frame-based and spectrometer references. Compared against the typical 15-minute acquisition time for step-scan reference configuration, both methods represent a speedup larger than 3 orders of magnitude, entering real-time acquisition speeds: the derivative-based method acquires the useful interferometric range in approximately 200 ms, corresponding to an effective rate of approximately 5 hyperspectral cubes per second, whereas the modulation-assisted method achieves an experimentally measured spectral resolution of 17.9 nm at an effective acquisition rate of 2.5 datacubes per second. Finally, reflection-mode imaging and motion-compensated reconstruction of moving particles further demonstrate spatially resolved spectral recovery beyond static transmission measurements, establishing the solution as a promising technology for continuous, wide-field hyperspectral imaging.

</details>


### [107] [DUV (257 nm) LIDT of Anti-Reflective Coatings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26181)
*Martin Mydlar,Hana Turcicova,Priyadarshani Narayanasamy,Mihai George Muresan,Ondrej Novak,Jan Vanda,Tomas Tolenis,Jan Brajer,Tomas Mocek*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文测试工业用深紫外增透窗口的激光诱导损伤阈值（LIDT），在257 nm（由Yb:YAG 1030 nm经两级倍频获得，效率13.4%）下开展1-on-1与S-on-1（至10^5脉冲）；离子束溅射Al2O3/SiO2镀膜样品LIDT最高，分别为0.73和0.5 J/cm²，并观察到色心、弹道损伤、分层、起泡等不同损伤形貌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业深紫外（DUV）加工需要增透窗口具备可量化的长期抗激光损伤能力，而现有数据多基于单脉冲或少量脉冲测试，缺乏对工业资格认证有代表性的长期LIDT稳定性评估，故需针对257 nm、长脉冲数条件下的镀膜窗口进行系统损伤阈值测试。

Method: 以Yb:YAG基频（1030 nm）经两级倍频（二次谐波再倍频至四次谐波，转换效率13.4%）获得257 nm输出；对不同增透膜窗口样品开展1-on-1与S-on-1（最长100000脉冲，作为工业最低资格的代表性条件）LIDT测试，并对比镀膜工艺与材料体系，同时进行损伤形貌表征。

Result: 离子束溅射（IBS）制备的Al2O3/SiO2增透膜样品表现最佳，1-on-1与S-on-1的LIDT分别为0.73 J/cm²和0.5 J/cm²。不同样品在1-on-1与S-on-1条件下呈现出多种损伤形貌，包括色心、弹道式损伤、膜层分层与起泡。

Conclusion: IBS沉积的Al2O3/SiO2增透膜在257 nm DUV波段具有最高的抗损伤能力，且在多脉冲（至10^5）条件下仍保持可用的LIDT水平，可作为工业DUV窗口的优选膜系；1-on-1与S-on-1的阈值差异及多样化损伤形貌说明长期辐照下的损伤机制（色心积累、界面失效等）与单脉冲机制不同，需分别评估。

Abstract: Laser-induced damage threshold in the deep ultraviolet (DUV) was used to perform tests on AR coated windows intended for industrial processes. The 257 nm wavelength was generated from Yb:YAG fundamental (1030 nm) and the long-term LIDT stability (up to 100000 shots) was selected as representative for minimal industrial qualification. Selected setup for the fourth harmonics generation consists of two stages, generation of second harmonic and subsequently the fourth harmonic with 13,4% conversation efficiency. In both 1-on-1 and S-on-1 experiments, the optics coated with Al2O3/SiO2 by ion beam sputtering technique demonstrated the highest LIDT value of 0.73 and 0.5 J/cm2 respectively. Various damage morphologies, color centers, ballistic damage, delamination, and blistering were observed across the samples with respect to the 1-on-1 and S-on-1.

</details>


### [108] [Hybrid Plasmon-Phonon Eigenmodes and Vortex States in a Graphene Ring on a Silica Substrate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26314)
*Yanxu Guo,Oleh Demianyk,Vladimir R. Tuz*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 研究石墨烯环-SiO2衬底体系中表面等离激元与光学声子杂化模，及其局域多极本征模与涡旋态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究石墨烯等离激元与红外活性光学声子耦合所形成的杂化表面激元，并利用几何约束实现多谱段局域本征模与结构化近场涡旋态。

Method: 解析推导杂化表面极化激元的色散分支，并结合全波数值模拟验证；对环状谐振器中的局域多极本征模进行谱域与空间场分布分析；以正交四极场的相干叠加构造涡旋态。

Result: 得到谱域分离的多族局域多极本征模，具确定方位角对称性；不同色散分支上的四极模具有几乎相同的归一化空间场分布，但谱位置与衰减特性显著不同；正交四极模叠加产生方位相位绕向相反的局域涡旋态。

Conclusion: 模态对称性由谐振器几何（准静态描述）决定，谱特性由色散材料响应决定，从而为深度亚波长石墨烯谐振腔中的多频带涡旋态与结构化近场调控提供物理基础。

Abstract: We investigate hybrid plasmon-phonon eigenmodes and localized electromagnetic vortex states in a graphene ring supported by a dispersive silica substrate. The interaction between graphene plasmons and infrared-active optical phonons of the substrate gives rise to distinct spectral branches of surface polaritons, whose dispersion characteristics are established analytically and validated by full-wave numerical simulations. Geometric confinement of these hybrid surface excitations produces spectrally separated families of localized multipolar eigenmodes with corresponding azimuthal symmetries. Particular attention is devoted to quadrupolar eigenstates, which exhibit nearly identical normalized spatial field distributions despite their substantial spectral separation and different attenuation characteristics. We demonstrate that coherent superposition of orthogonal quadrupolar field patterns gives rise to localized vortex states with opposite azimuthal phase winding. The preservation of the spatial eigenmode structure across distinct spectral branches is consistent with a quasistatic description in which the resonator geometry governs the modal symmetry, whereas the dispersive material response determines the spectral properties. These findings reveal a route toward controlling structured electromagnetic near fields through plasmon--phonon hybridization and establish a physical basis for multiband vortex states in deeply subwavelength graphene resonators.

</details>


### [109] [High-Power Short-Wavelength Frequency Comb from Coherent Radiation of Uncompressed Electron Beams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26377)
*Chenzhi Xu,Bingyang Yan,Hanxiang Yang,Jiawei Yan,Kai Li,Haixiao Deng*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出基于未压缩电子束相干波荡器辐射的短波长高功率频率梳方案，用光学频率梳作种子建立公共相位参考，并通过干扰两条激光诱导微聚束路径抑制束能涨落敏感性，端到端模拟实现148 nm真空紫外（约1 W，单齿约6 μW）与软X射线（约100 mW，单齿约100 nW）频率梳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短波长高功率频率梳可为精密测量与高分辨光谱学提供新手段，但现有方案在维持脉冲间相位相干性方面受电子束能量涨落限制。

Method: 以光学频率梳作为种子，为连续电子束中的微聚束提供公共相位参考，将相位相干性传递至短波长谐波辐射；利用不同激光诱导微聚束路径之间的干涉，降低谐波聚束相位对电子束能量涨落的敏感度；开展端到端（start-to-end）模拟。

Result: 模拟在真空紫外（148 nm）与软X射线波段实现频率梳产生，平均辐射功率分别约1 W与100 mW，单根梳齿功率约6 μW与100 nW。

Conclusion: 该方案为高功率、相位相干的短波长频率梳提供了可行路径，可服务于精密光谱学，包括囚禁^{229}Th离子核跃迁的搜寻。

Abstract: High-power frequency combs at short wavelengths would open new opportunities for precision measurements and high-resolution spectroscopy. Here, we propose a frequency comb based on coherent undulator radiation from uncompressed electron bunches. An optical frequency comb serves as the seed, establishing a common phase reference for microbunching in successive electron bunches and transferring its phase coherence to short-wavelength harmonic radiation. By exploiting interference between distinct laser-induced microbunching pathways, we reduce the sensitivity of the harmonic bunching phase to electron-beam energy fluctuations, a major challenge for maintaining interpulse coherence. Start-to-end simulations demonstrate frequency comb generation in the vacuum-ultraviolet (148 nm) and soft X-ray regimes, yielding average radiation powers of approximately 1 W and 100 mW, respectively, with approximately 6 $μ$W and 100 nW concentrated in individual comb teeth. These results establish a promising route toward high-power, phase-coherent short-wavelength frequency combs for precision spectroscopy, including searches for the nuclear transition in trapped $^{229}$Th ions.

</details>


### [110] [A photon-number-conserving multimode framework for pulse propagation dynamics across zero nonlinearity: Kerr-Raman cooperation and competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26387)
*Chao Mei,Weiye Huang,Junhong Yang,Tao Sun,Qian Gao,Jintao Fan,Peilong Yang,Xinzhu Sang,Jinhui Yuan*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文建立了守恒光子数、含完整拉曼响应且在零非线性频率处仍良定义的时间域多模GNLSE，并用于分析三模相干激发下光子在空间模式间的再分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模光纤中的拉曼散射会使脉冲谱向低频移动并在模式间转移光子；当脉冲谱跨越非线性系数变号（零非线性频率）时，常规GNLSE会给出非物理结果，而已有的光子数守恒形式或仅限单模、或排除拉曼散射。

Method: 推导含完整拉曼响应、光子数守恒且零非线性频率处不发散的时间域多模GNLSE；证明在窄带、恒定正非线性系数极限下退化为标准含瞬态Kerr与延迟拉曼项的多模GNLSE；再应用于三个相干激发模式，其谱跨越零非线性频率。

Result: Kerr与拉曼过程先协同后竞争：第一阶段二者共同向基模转移光子，分别贡献约2%与43%的初始总光子数；第二阶段Kerr使光子移出基模（约-27%），拉曼使其回流（约+15%）。基模光子占比由20%升至65%再降至53%。不改变模式光子布居的Kerr项仍可重塑场并增强拉曼转移；仅改变相对输入相位、即使模式谱相同，也能反转初始拉曼驱动的基模光子流。

Conclusion: 瞬态与延迟非线性响应及模间相位共同支配跨越零非线性频率处的光子再分配；所提出的光子数守恒多模框架在零非线性频率处依然适用。

Abstract: Raman scattering in multimode fibers shifts pulse spectra to lower frequencies and transfers photons between spatial modes. When the nonlinear coefficient changes sign within the pulse spectrum, the ordinary generalized nonlinear Schrödinger equation (GNLSE) can give unphysical predictions. Existing photon-number-conserving formulations either treat a single mode or exclude Raman scattering. We develop a time-domain multimode GNLSE that conserves photon number, includes the full Raman response and remains well defined at the zero-nonlinearity frequency. For narrowband pulses with a constant positive nonlinear coefficient, the framework reduces to the standard multimode GNLSE with instantaneous Kerr and delayed Raman terms. We then apply the framework to three coherently excited modes whose spectra shift across the zero-nonlinearity frequency. The Kerr and Raman processes first cooperate and then compete. In the first stage, both processes transfer photons into the fundamental mode, contributing about 2\% and 43\% of the initial total photon number. In the second stage, Kerr transfer drives photons out of the fundamental mode, while Raman transfer drives photons back, contributing about $-27\%$ and $+15\%$, respectively. Consequently, the fundamental-mode photon fraction rises from 20\% to 65\% and then falls to 53\%. Kerr terms that leave the modal photon populations unchanged still reshape the fields and thereby enhance Raman transfer. Moreover, changing only the relative input phases can reverse the initial Raman-driven flow into the fundamental mode, even for identical modal spectra. Taken together, the results show how instantaneous and delayed nonlinear responses, together with intermodal phase, govern photon redistribution across zero nonlinearity.

</details>


### [111] [Simulation-pretrained autoencoders versus PCA for compressing multimode fibre transmission matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26391)
*Callum J. Wilson,Terry Wright,Qing Yang,Zhong Wen,Michael P. Pound,George S. D. Gordon*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 论文基准比较置换等变自编码器与PCA对多模光纤传输矩阵(TM)的压缩表示能力：自编码器在极少或零实测TM时作为先验有优势，但作为压缩器不如PCA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模光纤成像需测量传输矩阵(TM)，但TM体积庞大且光纤一移动就须重测；若能获得可行的紧凑TM模型，可降低存储与重估成本，因此需探究TM能被压缩到什么程度、以及仅凭多少个实测TM即可重建。

Method: 以在仿真光纤上预训练、再适配实测TM的置换等变自编码器为对象，与主成分分析(PCA)进行基准对比；评估62倍压缩下的重建误差、聚焦增强率、以及对TM平移25像素、缩放10%的鲁棒性，并考察零/少样本先验效果及跨光纤迁移。

Result: 62倍压缩下，仅用2个实测TM适配即可将50μm光纤TM重建到理想聚焦增强率的98%，且在平移25像素或缩放10%时仍保持准确，而线性基迅速退化。但自编码器并非更好的压缩器：用全部163个真实TM拟合时PCA误差1.1%，自编码器为11.9%，且PCA从约10个TM起已更准确。自编码器的价值在于先验：无真实TM时误差29%，而同一仿真拟合的PCA为91%。跨光纤迁移仅部分有效。

Conclusion: 自编码器的优势场景是实测TM极少或缺失时的先验，而非压缩精度；最有价值但尚未测试的扩展是从少于完整TM的测量中恢复像素基TM，从而把预训练先验从压缩工具转变为节省测量的工具。

Abstract: Multimode optical fibres can image through a thin, flexible probe once their transmission matrix (TM) has been measured, but a TM is large and must be re-measured whenever the fibre moves. A compact model of feasible TMs would make storing and re-estimating them cheaper, so we ask how compactly TMs can be represented, and from how few measured TMs. Here we benchmark a permutation-equivariant autoencoder, pretrained on simulated fibres and adapted to measured TMs, against principal component analysis (PCA). At 62x compression, adapting the pretrained autoencoder with just two measured TMs reconstructs the TM of a 50um fibre well enough to focus light at 98% of the ideal enhancement, and it stays accurate when the TMs are shifted by 25 pixels or zoomed by 10%, which quickly degrades a linear basis. It is not, however, a better compressor: PCA is about ten times more accurate when fitted on all 163 real TMs (1.1% against 11.9% error on held-out TMs) and is already more accurate from ten. The autoencoder's value is as a prior when few or no real TMs exist: with none it reaches 29% error, against 91% for PCA fitted on the same simulation. Transfer to a different fibre is only partial. The most important extension, not yet tested, is to recover pixel-basis TMs from fewer measurements than a full TM, which would turn the pretrained prior from a compressor into a measurement-saving tool.

</details>


### [112] [Full Kummer Beams: Skyrmionic textures in bichromatic light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26530)
*Jefferson Delgado-Quesada,Miguel A. Alonso,Eileen Otte*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出"完整Kummer光束"，即完整Poincaré光束的双色类比，用三个参数刻画双色偏振态并张成Kummer曲面；在特定条件下该类光束可呈现Skyrme子纹理，其Skyrme数同时依赖拓扑荷与频率，并展现出鲁棒的类高阶结构及在宽束腰范围内的传播不变Skyrme数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完整Poincaré光束（full Poincaré beams）已在单色情形下被广泛研究，用于在横截面上遍历Poincaré球面上的全部偏振态。作者希望将其推广到双色（bichromatic）情形，借助Kummer曲面的几何结构来刻画双色偏振态，从而探索双色光场中新的拓扑与Skyrme子（skyrmionic）结构。

Method: 定义三个参数来表征双色偏振态，并以其张成"Kummer曲面"（Poincaré球面的双色对应物）；在此基础上构造完整Kummer光束，分析其Skyrme子纹理的拓扑性质，考察Skyrme数对拓扑荷与频率的依赖，并研究其在传播过程中的演化行为（包括类高阶结构与传播不变性）。

Result: 发现在特定条件下完整Kummer光束可具有Skyrme子纹理，且其Skyrme数不仅取决于拓扑荷，还取决于频率；光束在传播中表现出涌现行为，包括鲁棒的、类高阶的结构，以及在较大范围的束腰选择下保持传播不变的Skyrme数。

Conclusion: 完整Kummer光束将完整Poincaré光束的偏振覆盖思想扩展到双色领域，提供了以Kummer曲面为几何框架研究双色偏振拓扑的新途径，并揭示了频率作为额外调控自由度对Skyrme数的关键作用及传播鲁棒的拓扑结构，为结构化光场的拓扑调控提供了新思路。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce full Kummer beams, the bichromatic analog of full Poincaré beams. To this end, we define three parameters that characterize bichromatic states of polarization and span the Kummer shape, the bichromatic counterpart of the Poincaré sphere. We find that, under certain conditions, full Kummer beams can exhibit skyrmionic textures with a Skyrme number that depends not only on the topological charges but also on the frequencies. These beams can also exhibit emergent behavior upon propagation, including robust, higher-order-like structures and a propagation-invariant Skyrme number over a wide range of beam-waist selections.

</details>


### [113] [From power to spectrum: an off-the-shelf photodiode turned into a spectrometer by its bias alone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26532)
*Roi Yozevitch,Shmuel Sternklar,Ziv Glasser*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出免制造的通用光谱仪方案：仅靠电偏置调制标准商用光电二极管，利用偏置调制光电色散（OED）效应，将光谱编码进光电流波形，配合神经网络解码，实现0.3–0.4 nm谱线定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算光谱仪虽以可变谱响应的探测器替代色散光学元件，但每类器件都需专门制造（二维异质结、渐变外延结、有机叠层、滤光片或光子电路），成本高、通用性差，缺乏一种无需改动器件即可获得波长分辨能力的通用途径。

Method: 基于偏置调制光电色散（OED）机制：不同波长光在半导体中吸收深度不同，正弦偏置改变各深度产生载流子的收集效率，使一个偏置周期内的光电流波形形状编码完整光谱。实验采用未经改写的商用InGaAs PIN光电二极管，室温下采集10 μs波形，用神经网络从波形反演光谱；无需光学滤光片，也无需对入射光做调制。

Result: 在C+L波段80 nm范围内重建1–3条激光谱线：谱线定位误差0.3–0.4 nm，约99%的测量中正确计数谱线数目，可识别各线功率水平，谱线漂移可追踪至数十皮米量级。

Conclusion: OED源于普适的半导体输运物理，方案材料无关，原则上适用于任何现成光电二极管，可把已部署的功率监测探测器转化为低成本光谱传感器，为免制造、免滤光、免光调制的通用计算光谱学提供新范式。

Abstract: Computational spectrometers replace dispersive optics with detectors whose spectral response can be varied, but every reported single-detector device obtains this response through dedicated fabrication: two-dimensional heterostructures, graded epitaxial junctions, organic stacks, filters or photonic circuits. We demonstrate a generic, fabrication-free alternative in which a standard commercial photodiode becomes a spectrometer through its electrical drive alone, requiring no modification of the device, no optical filter and no modulation of the light. The mechanism is bias-modulated optoelectronic chromatic dispersion (OED): light of different wavelengths is absorbed at different depths, and a sinusoidal bias changes how carriers generated at each depth are collected, so that every spectrum is encoded in the shape of the photocurrent waveform over one bias period. Applied to an unmodified InGaAs PIN photodiode at room temperature, a 10 us waveform decoded by a neural network reconstructs spectra of one to three laser lines across the 80 nm of the telecom C+L band, locating the lines to 0.3-0.4 nm, counting them in about 99% of measurements and identifying their power levels, with line shifts tracked to tens of picometres. Because OED relies on universal semiconductor transport physics, the approach is material-agnostic and applies in principle to any off-the-shelf photodiode, turning detectors already deployed as power monitors into low-cost spectral sensors.

</details>
